The benchmark comprises 3,920 expert-verified question-answer pairs derived from 890 patients across over 3,200 longitudinal MRI timepoints, drawn from seven clinical cohorts covering glioblastoma, neurodegeneration, vestibular schwannoma, and brain metastases, in open-ended, multiple-choice, and binary formats, requiring models to identify anatomical regions of maximal change, characterize progression across sequences and views, and provide structured guidance specifying boundaries, imaging features, and confounders. Experiments across 16 vision-language models reveal moderate temporal alignment but systematic failure on change direction recognition and volumetric quantification, while multi-view inputs improve spatial localization yet degrade temporal reasoning in compact architectures. Our benchmark provides a systematic framework for evaluating progression tracking, interval change localization, and temporal ordering, which are essential for clinical deployment. Code, evaluation splits, and the dataset are available at: https://github.com/wafaAlghallabi/Time-Aware-MRI.

</details>


### [70] [AmalthAI: An Open-Source Computer Vision Platform for Cultural Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13343)
*Christos Chatzisavvas,Stelios Alvanos,Efstratios Politis,Panagiotis Rigas,Thomas Pappas,Ioannis Giannoukos,Nikolaos Mitianoudis,Agata Ulanowska,Katarzyna Żebrowska,Nazarij Buławka,Christina Margariti,George Pavlidis,Chairi Kiourt,Anestis Koutsoudis,Vassilis Katsouros,George Ioannakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AmalthAI是一个开源的计算机视觉平台，旨在让缺乏机器学习背景的文化遗产领域专家能够独立完成数据集管理、模型训练、推理和验证。平台支持分类、分割和目标检测任务，集成Kubeflow/Katib进行可扩展训练与超参数搜索，通过Grad-CAM和视觉语言模型提供预测解释，并支持本地部署以保障敏感考古数据安全。在粘土纺织印迹数据集上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉和机器学习虽然为文化遗产文物分析提供了新工具，但CV/ML流水线对文化遗产领域专家而言难以使用，他们缺乏配置、训练和评估模型所需的专业知识背景，这形成了技术工具与领域专家之间的巨大鸿沟。

Method: 开发了一个名为AmalthAI的开源CV平台，包含数据集管理、训练和推理功能，支持分类、分割和目标检测任务。利用Kubeflow和Katib实现可扩展训练与超参数搜索；使用Grad-CAM定位预测所依据的图像区域，并借助视觉语言模型(VLM)生成文本描述供专家审查。平台支持自主托管部署，确保涉及国家所有权或权限受限的考古数据不离开机构内部。

Result: 平台在基于粘土纺织印迹自定义数据集的考古用例上进行了测试，文化遗产专家成功完成了分割和分类模型的训练与验证，并用于假设检验。实现代码已在GitHub上开源提供。

Conclusion: AmalthAI有效弥合了文化遗产领域专家与CV/ML技术之间的鸿沟，使非机器学习专业背景的考古专家能够独立产出和验证具有考古学意义的研究发现，同时通过本地化部署解决敏感数据合规问题。

Abstract: Computer vision (CV) and machine learning (ML) offer new tools for cultural heritage (CH) artifact analysis, but the CV/ML pipeline remains largely inaccessible to CH domain experts, who lack the background to configure, train, or assess models. We present AmalthAI, an open-source CV platform that bridges this gap, enabling non-ML CH experts to independently produce and validate archaeologically meaningful findings. The interface covers dataset management, training, and inference for classification, segmentation, and object detection, with Kubeflow and Katib handling scalable training and hyperparameter search. Grad-CAM localizes the image region behind a prediction, and a vision-language model (VLM) adds a text description of it for expert review. Since archaeological data is often state-owned or rights-encumbered and cannot leave institutional custody, AmalthAI's self-hostable deployment ensures sensitive data is kept within premises. We test the platform on an archaeological use case built on a custom dataset of clay textile imprints, where CH experts trained and validated segmentation, and classification models for hypothesis testing. We provide the implementation code at https://github.com/TEXTaiLES/AmalthAI.

</details>


### [71] [Sign Language Video Synthesis via Loss-Guided Multi-Expert GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13368)
*Dingzhan Nong,Zhihao Ren,Ziqi Li,Tim Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于损失引导的多专家生成对抗网络（GAN）框架，用于手语视频合成。通过全局、手部、头部三个专用判别器引导生成器中对应专家分支，并引入United Loss共识机制（10%权重向集成平均正则化）稳定多判别器训练。采用双路径卷积-Transformer架构和自适应特征融合，结合三种模式的交替训练策略。0.2B参数变体达到29.8 PSNR，1.3B变体达到30.7 PSNR，推理显存仅需1.5GB和8GB，可在消费级硬件上部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为改善听障人士的沟通体验，需要合成高质量的手语视频。现有方法在视觉区域特征专业化方面存在不足，且多判别器GAN系统在早期训练阶段存在混沌动力学问题，亟需一个能够隐式实现特征专业化且训练稳定的框架。

Method: 提出多专家GAN框架，包含三个专用判别器（全局、手、头），每个引导生成器对应专家分支实现隐式特征专业化。引入United Loss共识机制（10%权重）稳定多判别器训练；每个分支采用双路径卷积-Transformer设计，配备可学习的自适应特征融合模块；采用三模式交替训练策略（判别器、整体生成、分支专门生成）。

Result: 在自定义156GB数据集（含过滤掉简单和重复样本的测试集）上，0.2B参数变体达29.8 PSNR，1.3B参数变体达30.7 PSNR；推理显存分别为1.5GB和8GB，可在消费级硬件上部署。系统于2025年香港前沿技术峰会展出。

Conclusion: 该框架通过多专家分支和United Loss机制有效实现了手语视频合成中不同视觉区域的特征专业化与训练稳定性，同时在小参数规模下达到高PSNR，具备消费级硬件部署可行性。完整消融研究因单个GPU上2-3个月的训练周期仍在进行中。

Abstract: This preliminary technical report presents a framework for sign language video synthesis using a loss-guided multi-expert Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) to enhance communication for individuals with hearing impairments. Three specialized discriminators -- global, hand, and head -- each guide a corresponding expert branch in the generator toward a distinct visual region, enabling implicit feature specialization without explicit diversity losses. To stabilize this multi-discriminator system, whose early-phase training otherwise exhibits chaotic dynamics, we introduce a United Loss consensus mechanism that regularizes each discriminator toward the ensemble average at a 10% weight. Each branch further adopts a dual-pathway convolutional-transformer design with learnable AdaptiveFeatureFusion, balancing the stability of convolutions against the detail of windowed self-attention. The generator is trained using an alternating three-mode schedule (discriminator, holistic generation, branch-specialized generation). On a custom 156GB dataset with a filtered test set that removes easy and repetitive samples, our 0.2B-parameter variant achieves 29.8 PSNR and the 1.3B-parameter variant achieves 30.7 PSNR, with inference VRAM footprints of 1.5 GB and 8 GB respectively, enabling deployment on consumer-grade hardware. Full ablation studies remain ongoing due to the 2-3 month training cycle on a single GPU. The system was showcased at the 2025 Hong Kong Frontier Technology Summit.

</details>


### [72] [Reconstructing Historical Manuscripts through MSI: The Potential of Contrast in Assessing Image Quality and Legibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13381)
*Anna Breger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于对比度的图像质量度量在历史手稿多光谱重建图像质量评估中的适用性。实验表明，潜在对比度与专家评分相关性最高，而对比度噪声比与全参考质量度量一致性最强，且对比度类度量整体优于通用图像质量度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史手稿数字修复旨在提高可读性同时保留文化遗产的真实性，但重建手稿图像的质量评估面临挑战：可读性主观性强且专家标注稀缺，需要客观、有效的质量评估指标。

Method: 利用公开数据集开展两项实验，以专家手动质量评分和全参考图像质量度量作为参考评估，系统比较各类基于对比度的图像质量度量与传统通用图像质量度量在手稿重建图像评价中的表现。

Result: 潜在对比度与专家评分的相关性最高；对比度噪声比在全参考质量度量方面的一致性最强；基于对比度的度量整体优于通用图像质量度量。

Conclusion: 基于对比度的质量度量可作为手稿可读性和重建质量的客观指标，为历史手稿数字修复的质量评估提供了有效、可行的客观评价方案。

Abstract: Digital restoration of historical manuscript images aims to improve readability while preserving the authenticity of cultural heritage documents. However, evaluating quality of restored manuscripts remains challenging, where readability is often subjective and expert annotations are scarce. This study investigates the suitability of contrast-based image quality measures to assess quality and legibility of reconstructed manuscript images from multi-spectral imaging. Two experiments were conducted with publicly-available data sets, facilitating manual quality scores by experts and full-reference image quality measures as reference evaluations. The results show that potential contrast achieves the highest correlation with expert ratings, while contrast-to-noise ratio demonstrates the strongest agreement with full-reference quality measures. Overall, contrast-based measures consistently outperform general image quality measures, demonstrating their potential as objective indicators of manuscript legibility and reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [73] [When Is a Task Vector Enough? An Empirical Theory of Implicit Multimodal ICL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13385)
*Jiaqian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"选择-实现假设"（Selection–Realization Hypothesis），用于解释多模态上下文中隐式推理（M-ICL）何时可以压缩为静态任务向量、何时需要更复杂的干预机制。通过控制查询依赖度的对照实验，发现静态任务向量的成功与跨查询共享的演示诱导变化程度密切相关，而查询特定或分布式的结构则需要更复杂的干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态上下文学习中的隐式推理方法在如何依赖于查询以及如何修改模型方面存在显著差异，但尚不清楚不同复杂度的干预机制在何种任务条件下是必要的，缺乏统一的理论框架来指导方法选择。

Method: 提出Selection–Realization Hypothesis，通过受控多模态任务评估该假设，其中查询依赖性在不改变底层任务原语和提示格式的情况下变化。通过将正确演示与匹配的反事实样例进行对比，测量显式M-ICL的结构并检验其是否能预测干预行为。

Result: 静态任务向量的成功与演示诱导变化的跨查询共享程度密切相关；当显式M-ICL包含查询特定或分布式结构而局部加性偏移无法恢复时，额外的干预复杂度变得有用。这些关系在自然VQA基准上同样成立，支持无需测试性能的成本感知方法选择。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个统一的实证理论，阐明何时演示可以被压缩为任务向量以及何时需要更具表达力的干预机制，为多模态上下文学习中的方法选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Implicit multimodal in-context learning compresses demonstrations into internal interventions, ranging from static task vectors to query-conditioned transformations and attention routing. Despite their common goal, these methods differ substantially in how the intervention depends on the query and where it modifies the model, leaving unclear which additional complexity is necessary for a given task. We propose the Selection--Realization Hypothesis. It views demonstrations as inducing a compact family of internal changes from which the query selects, while the model's computation constrains how the selected change can be implemented. We evaluate this account using controlled multimodal tasks in which query dependence varies without changing the underlying task primitives or prompt format. By contrasting correct demonstrations with matched counterfactuals, we measure the structure of explicit M-ICL and test whether it predicts intervention behavior. We find that the success of a static task vector is closely tied to how much of the demonstration-induced change is shared across queries. Additional intervention complexity becomes useful when explicit M-ICL contains query-specific or distributed structure that a local additive shift cannot recover. These relationships extend to natural VQA benchmarks and support cost-aware method selection without access to test performance. Our results provide a unified empirical theory of when demonstrations can be compressed into a task vector and when a more expressive intervention is warranted.

</details>


### [74] [Context-Matched Distillation: Teacher Causality for Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13391)
*Hmrishav Bandyopadhyay,Xuanchi Ren,Zijian Huang,Jay Zhangjie Wu,Tianshi Cao,Ruilong Li,Bryan Chu,Sanja Fidler,Yi-Zhe Song,Zian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文匹配蒸馏（CMD），一种用于自回归视频生成的因果分布匹配蒸馏框架。CMD通过使用因果教师模型替代双向全片段评分，使教师监督与学生生成目标时可用的因果信息对齐，并配合前缀评分与前缀扰动策略，实现低延迟的少步蒸馏视频生成，在短/长视频基准上达到最优聚合性能，并显著提升对时变相机控制的遵循度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）流程中，少步因果学生模型由双向教师进行监督，教师评分完整片段时可能依赖目标生成时学生不可得的未来帧和控制信号，导致教师监督与学生因果信息集不一致，制约了自回归在线视频生成的准确性和可控性。

Method: CMD包含三个核心组件：(1) 用因果教师替代双向全片段评分，每个目标被评估时不访问未来帧或控制信号；(2) 前缀评分（Prefix Scoring），在缓存的学生生成前缀下评估每个目标，使监督与实际生成上下文对齐；(3) 前缀扰动（Prefix Corruption），通过扰动训练早期产生的不稳定前缀来稳定训练。该方法自然扩展到帧级/块级生成、长视频蒸馏和相机条件蒸馏。

Result: 实验表明CMD在短视频和长视频基准上均达到自回归方法中的最优聚合性能，并显著提升了对时变相机控制的遵循能力。

Conclusion: 通过在整个教师训练、学生蒸馏和推理阶段统一因果信息边界，CMD实现了上下文匹配的因果蒸馏，兼顾了低延迟生成、时间一致性和在线可控性，为自回归视频生成提供了一种简洁而有效的范式。

Abstract: Interactive autoregressive video generation demands both low-latency rollouts and precise online control. Few-step distillation accelerates generation by reducing denoising steps, while online control imposes a causal constraint: frames and blocks should depend on history and controls available during generation. Existing video distribution matching distillation (DMD) pipelines, however, often supervise causal few-step students using bidirectional teachers that score complete clips. The score for a target can therefore depend on future frames and controls that were unavailable when the student generated it, misaligning teacher supervision with the student's causal information set. We introduce Context-Matched Distillation (CMD), a causal DMD framework that aligns teacher supervision with the information available when each target is generated. CMD replaces bidirectional full-clip scoring with a causal teacher that evaluates each target without access to future frames or controls. The same causal teacher initializes the few-step student, establishing a consistent causal formulation across teacher training, student distillation, and inference. Beyond aligning the temporal information boundary, Prefix Scoring matches supervision to the student's realized rollout context by evaluating each target under the cached student-generated prefix that produced it. Prefix Corruption further stabilizes training by perturbing unreliable prefixes produced early in training while preserving this target-context alignment. With a simple causal formulation, CMD naturally extends to frame-wise and chunk-wise generation, long video distillation, and camera-conditioned distillation. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art aggregate performance among autoregressive methods on both short- and long-video benchmarks, together with substantially improved adherence to time-varying camera controls.

</details>


### [75] [StreamTTT: Reconciling Real-Time Perception and Long-Term Memory in Streaming VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13416)
*Joya Chen,Zeyun Zhong,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamTTT通过将长时历史写入注意力上下文之外的在线更新快速权重，保留短滑动键值缓存用于近期证据，从而在流式视频理解中同时兼顾实时感知与长期记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频大模型（VLMs）在实时感知和长期记忆之间存在权衡——缩短上下文可提升当前场景感知但损害长程回忆，现有方法难以同时兼顾两者。

Method: 提出StreamTTT，将长时历史编码进在线更新的快速权重（不属于注意力上下文），同时保留一个较短的滑动键值缓存用于近期证据，以减少注意力稀释。在离线长视频QA和新建的实时QA语料上联合训练。

Result: 在OVO-Bench上，StreamTTT-4B相比SimpleStream-4B在实时感知上提升1.4分，在回溯追踪上提升3.7分；在StreamingBench的RTVU子集上与更大的SimpleStream-8B保持竞争力。

Conclusion: StreamTTT证明了将长期记忆移出注意力上下文并采用快速权重策略可以有效调和实时感知与长期记忆之间的矛盾，代码将公开。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly perceive the present while remembering the past, yet streaming VLMs often trade off real-time perception against long-term memory. Prior work shows that shortening the context can sharpen current-scene perception at the expense of long-range recall. To reconcile these abilities, we introduce StreamTTT, which writes long-range history into online-updated fast weights outside the attention context. This leaves a short sliding key-value cache dedicated to recent evidence, mitigating attention dilution. We train StreamTTT jointly on offline long-video QA and a newly constructed real-time QA corpus. On OVO-Bench, StreamTTT-4B outperforms SimpleStream-4B by 1.4 points in real-time perception and 3.7 points in backward tracing. It also remains competitive with the larger SimpleStream-8B on the Real-Time Visual Understanding (RTVU) subset of StreamingBench. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [76] [Edit2TikZ: A Comprehensive and Challenging Benchmark for Scientific Figure Editing with TikZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13441)
*Zongyun Zhang,Jiacheng Ruan,Xian Gao,Ruizhu Zhou,Lingcheng Meng,Lining Hu,Ting Liu,Yuzhuo Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Edit2TikZ，一个用于科学图表代码编辑任务的全面基准，包含1,548个高质量样本，覆盖多步编辑与不同的定位请求方式。通过评估14个主流MLLM发现现有模型在此任务上表现不可靠，为此构建TikZEditMix训练集并采用重建-再编辑的课程学习方法，将Qwen3.5-4B的编译成功率从45.35%提升至83.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TikZ基准主要关注图表重建和生成，缺乏对指令引导的科学图表编辑任务的系统评估。科学图表编辑比生成更具挑战性，需要模型同时恢复视觉结构、理解修改请求、生成可编译代码并保留无关内容，这一关键任务尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 构建Edit2TikZ基准，包含1,548个样本，结合真实世界和受控合成编辑案例，支持文本和视觉两种定位请求方式，并提供逐步注释的多步编辑。同时设计人类对齐的评估框架用于衡量编辑完成度与无关内容保留程度。此外，构建TikZEditMix混合训练集，采用重建-再编辑（reconstruction-then-editing）的课程学习方法训练紧凑模型。

Result: 评估14个主流MLLM后发现：专有模型平均编译成功率仅为75%，在图表恢复和编辑正确性方面表现有限；低于9B的紧凑模型在指令跟随和完整图表生成上表现更差。通过所提训练方法，Qwen3.5-4B的编译成功率从45.35%提升至83.40%，在所提出的评估指标上平均提升18.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 科学图表编辑对当前MLLM而言仍是高度不可靠的任务，但通过针对性的混合训练数据和课程学习策略可以显著提升紧凑模型的能力，为该研究方向提供了基准、评估框架和训练方法的完整体系。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown substantial potential in visual understanding and graphic code generation, editing scientific figures through code presents a greater challenge: a model must jointly recover visual structure, ground the requested change, generate compilable code, and preserve all unrelated content. While existing TikZ benchmarks mainly focus on figure reconstruction and generation, few systematically evaluate instruction-guided scientific figure editing with compilable code. We introduce Edit2TikZ, a comprehensive benchmark for scientific figure editing tasks, featuring 1,548 diverse and high-quality samples. Edit2TikZ combines real-world and controlled synthetic edit cases, supports both textual and visual localization request, and contains multi-step editing, each with step-level annotations. We further construct a human-aligned evaluation framework to measure whether a requested edit is completed while irrelevant content is preserved. Utilizing Edit2TikZ, we evaluate 14 mainstream MLLMs and find that current systems remain unreliable: on average, proprietary models achieve a compilation success rate of merely 75% and remain limited in both figure restoration and edit correctness, while compact models below 9B struggle further with instruction following and complete figure generation. Therefore, we build a mixed training set TikZEditMix and adopt reconstruction-then-editing curriculum learning for compact models. On Qwen3.5-4B, this training improves the compilation success rate from 45.35% to 83.40% and yields an average improvement of 18.7 points across our proposed evaluation metrics. The code and data will be released at https://github.com/Solunny/Edit2TikZ.

</details>


### [77] [UniTexture: Cross-Task Universal Adversarial Textures for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13453)
*Yukun Dai,Mingzhe Dai,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniTexture，一种跨任务通用的对抗性纹理攻击方法，通过单一纹理3D物体即可在多任务VLA模型上诱导目标动作偏差，大幅降低任务成功率并展现跨模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人策略攻击方法通常针对单一任务或指令进行优化，多任务VLA模型的跨任务脆弱性尚未得到充分探索，缺乏能够通过单一攻击向量同时影响多个任务的研究。

Method: UniTexture通过可微渲染器将策略动作输出的梯度反向传播至表面纹理参数，在任务、指令、状态和视点的分布上联合优化共享纹理，使用目标动作空间目标函数将预测动作引向攻击者定义的目标，无需为每个任务单独优化纹理。

Result: 在OpenVLA和π0.5上评估，UniTexture将平均任务成功率从良性条件下的90.0%降至攻击下的48.4%，诱导目标对齐的动作偏移，并展现跨套件和跨模型的迁移能力，无需重新优化。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多任务VLA中存在共享的跨任务脆弱性，可通过单一对抗性表面纹理被系统性利用，为VLA模型的安全性研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as generalist robotic policies capable of following diverse language instructions and performing a wide range of manipulation tasks. However, their direct control over embodied agents also exposes them to adversarial interference that may cause unsafe physical behaviors. Existing attacks on robotic policies are typically optimized for a single task or instruction, leaving the cross-task vulnerabilities of multitask VLAs largely unexplored. We introduce UniTexture, a cross-task universal adversarial texture attack that uses a single textured 3D object to induce targeted deviations in VLA action predictions across multiple tasks. UniTexture backpropagates gradients from the policy's action outputs to surface texture parameters through a differentiable renderer. It jointly optimizes the shared texture over a distribution of tasks, instructions, states, and viewpoints using a targeted action-space objective, steering predicted actions toward attacker-defined targets without optimizing a separate texture for each task. We evaluate UniTexture on OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$ across diverse manipulation tasks and multiple evaluation settings. UniTexture reduces the mean task success rate from 90.0% under benign conditions to 48.4% under attack, induces target-aligned action shifts, and further exhibits cross-suite and cross-model transfer without re-optimization. Together, these findings reveal shared cross-task vulnerabilities in multitask VLAs that can be systematically exploited through a single adversarial surface texture.

</details>


### [78] [Evaluation of Clinically Steerable Retinal Image Generation from Foundation Model Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13455)
*Zuzanna A. Wakefield-Skórniewska,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种视网膜基础模型在表示分词器框架下的可控图像生成能力，发现生成的表示和图像能忠实继承表型信息，在源自基础模型的下游任务上优于传统潜扩散模型，但在基于真实图像训练的分类器上性能优势消失，揭示了合成到真实的表示差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型能够学习临床表型的潜在表示，但其是否支持可控图像生成尚未被充分探索，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在表示分词器框架下评估四种视网膜基础模型，测试潜在表示中的人口统计学和临床信息是否能在合成图像生成过程中被保留，并与传统潜扩散模型进行对比。

Result: 生成的表示和图像在源基础模型内能忠实继承表型信息，并在多个下游预测任务上优于传统潜扩散模型；但在使用真实图像训练的分类器评估时优势消失，揭示了合成到真实表示差距。

Conclusion: 基础模型潜空间为可控视网膜合成提供了强大基础，但需要更好地对齐合成表示与真实图像分布。

Abstract: Medical foundation models learn latent representations of clinically meaningful phenotypes, yet their ability to support controllable image generation remains largely unexplored. We evaluate four retinal foundation models within the representation tokenizer framework and examine whether demographic and clinical information encoded in latent representations from foundation models is preserved during synthetic image generation. We show that generated representations and images faithfully inherit phenotype information when evaluated within their originating foundation models, consistently outperforming conventional latent diffusion on multiple downstream prediction tasks. However, these gains largely disappear when evaluated using classifiers trained on real images, revealing a previously uncharacterised synthetic-to-real representation gap. These findings demonstrate that foundation-model latent spaces provide a powerful substrate for controllable retinal synthesis while highlighting the need to better align synthetic representations with real-image distributions.

</details>


### [79] [Fine-Grained Action Recognition with Cross-Attentive Latent Sparse Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13458)
*Imtiaz Ul Hassan,Tasweer Ahmad,Nik Bessis,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FineX是一个用于细粒度人体动作识别的多模态融合框架，通过将RGB外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑三种表征进行分解，利用成对交叉注意力实现对称的信息交换，并采用流式潜稀疏混合专家（MoE）进行条件化专家路由，在Gym99、Gym288和Diving48数据集上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度人体动作识别（FHAR）需要区分仅在身体姿态、时序或局部外观上存在细微差异的相似动作。RGB表征保留了视觉上下文但缺乏关节级几何信息，而骨骼表征编码了运动学但丢失了密集空间细节。现有单一模态表示不足以充分刻画细粒度动作的判别性特征。

Method: FineX将细粒度线索分解为三种表征流：RGB外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑。采用成对交叉注意力实现对称且保持流信息的信息交换机制；随后引入流式潜稀疏混合专家（MoE）模块，将每种表征路由到内容相关的共享专家子集，并通过负载均衡目标进行正则化。

Result: FineX在Gym99、Gym288和Diving48数据集上达到最先进水平。在长尾分布的Gym288数据集上，平均类别准确率从68.6%提升至76.2%（+7.6个百分点）。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了结构化视觉-姿态-图融合以及条件专家精化对细粒度人体动作识别的有效性，并且无需文本监督或大规模视觉-语言预训练即可取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Fine-grained human action recognition (FHAR) must distinguish visually similar actions that differ mainly in body configuration, timing, or local appearance. RGB representations retain visual context but often suppress joint-level geometry, whereas skeleton representations encode kinematics but discard dense spatial detail. We introduce FineX, which factorizes fine-grained cues into RGB appearance, pose heatmap geometry, and skeletal-graph topology. Pairwise cross-attention enables symmetric, stream-preserving information exchange, followed by a streamwise latent sparse Mixture-of-Experts that routes each representation to a content-dependent subset of shared experts, regularized by a load-balancing objective. FineX achieves state-of-the-art results on Gym99, Gym288, and Diving48. On the long-tailed Gym288, it raises mean class accuracy from 68.6% to 76.2% (+7.6 points) without textual supervision or large-scale vision-language pre-training, demonstrating the benefit of structured visual-pose-graph fusion and conditional expert refinement for FHAR.

</details>


### [80] [SNM-VFI: Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-Guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13460)
*Jisoo Jeong,Hong Cai,Jamie Menjay Lin,Hanno Ackermann,Hyeonjun Sim,Yinhao Zhu,Yunxiao Shi,Fatih Porikli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SNM-VFI，一种无需训练的框架，结合预训练光流模型与视频扩散模型，通过对称非线性运动模型生成对应感知的中间帧，实现运动可控的视频帧插值，在DAVIS、Sintel和KITTI基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩散模型的视频帧插值方法从随机噪声合成中间帧，难以保持密集运动对应关系。作者旨在解决生成过程中运动一致性问题，同时兼顾感知真实感和重建精度。

Method: 首先利用预训练光流模型构建多帧非线性光流中间帧和置信度图；然后将光流引导的帧编码为潜在先验，初始化和迭代引导预训练视频扩散模型；最后利用置信度图融合结构可靠的光流预测与扩散生成的不确定区域（如遮挡和物体边界）细节。

Result: 在DAVIS、Sintel和KITTI等挑战性基准上，SNM-VFI在感知质量、重建精度和时间连贯性方面均取得优异表现，验证了其在不同运动场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SNM-VFI通过对称非线性运动模型将光流信息嵌入扩散生成过程，在保持密集运动对应的同时提升感知真实感，为生成式视频帧插值提供了一种无需训练的可行方案。

Abstract: We propose Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation (SNM-VFI), a training-free framework for motion-controllable generative video frame interpolation with pre-trained optical flow and video diffusion models. Unlike conventional diffusion-based VFI methods that synthesize intermediate frames from random noise, SNM-VFI guides the generative process with correspondence-aware frames produced by a symmetric nonlinear motion model. Specifically, we first utilize a pre-trained optical flow model to construct multi-frame nonlinear flow-based intermediate frames and confidence maps. These flow-guided frames are then encoded as latent priors to initialize and iteratively guide a pre-trained Video Diffusion model, enabling the diffusion model to preserve dense motion correspondence while improving perceptual realism. To further enhance output quality, we employ confidence maps to fuse structurally reliable flow-based predictions with diffusion-generated details in uncertain regions such as occlusions and object boundaries. Extensive evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including DAVIS, Sintel, and KITTI, demonstrate that SNM-VFI achieves strong perceptual quality, competitive reconstruction accuracy, and robust temporal coherence across diverse motion scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [MLLM-Routed Heterogeneous Ensembles for Robust Cross-Dataset Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13463)
*Daniel Perkins,John Squires,Janou Milligan,Chandra Raskoti,Linda Ungerboeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARMDIL，一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）代理的自适应路由框架，用于多领域图像分类。它通过将每张图像动态路由到最合适的视觉骨干网络（包括CNN、自监督表示学习模型和视觉语言模型），在统一标签空间上实现跨领域图像分类，并通过自然语言推理轨迹增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像分类模型在单一任务特定数据集上表现优异，但难以跨领域和难度级别泛化。不同视觉架构（CNN、SSL、VLM）各有优缺点和适用场景，需要一种智能机制来动态选择最适合的模型。

Method: ARMDIL采用MLLM代理作为路由器，将每张输入图像动态路由到最合适的视觉骨干网络。其集成成员包括ResNet（卷积神经网络）、SSL（自监督表示学习器）和VLM（视觉语言模型），每个模型都在由多个图像数据集构建的统一标签空间上训练。

Result: 实验评估揭示了不同架构在不同视觉领域的独特能力和脆弱性。ARMDIL能有效权衡这些优缺点，性能与专门的训练型路由器相当，同时通过简单提示修改即可整合新信息，大幅提升适应性，并通过自然语言推理轨迹增强可解释性。

Conclusion: ARMDIL为跨数据集图像分类提供了更可靠、更通用的视觉系统解决方案，为AI助手和自主机器人等通用视觉系统的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Modern image classification models excel when trained on single task-specific datasets but often struggle to generalize across domains and difficulty levels. We propose ARMDIL, an Adaptive Router for Multi-Domain Image classification with LLMs. ARMDIL is an ensemble that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) agent to dynamically route each image to the most suitable vision backbone. Our diverse ensemble employs convolutional neural networks (ResNets), self-supervised representation learners (SSL), and vision-language models (VLMs), each trained on a unified label space constructed from multiple image datasets with differing distributions and characteristics. Empirical evaluations illuminate the distinct capabilities and vulnerabilities of each architecture across disparate visual domains. Crucially, we show that ARMDIL effectively navigates these trade-offs, performing competitively with specialized training-based routers. Furthermore, it drastically improves adaptability by allowing new information to be integrated via simple prompt modifications, while enhancing interpretability through natural language reasoning traces. These advances in cross-dataset image classification pave the way for more reliable general-purpose vision systems such as AI assistants and autonomous robots.

</details>


### [82] [MapRoute++: Surrogate-Guided Semantic Routing for Visual Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13478)
*Ashok Urlana,L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Vivek Hruday Kavuri,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MapRoute的视觉概念遗忘方法，通过引入任务特定训练目标、更丰富的概念表示和语义路由机制，在Genμ 2.0挑战赛任务3中实现了概念的有效消除，同时保留无关和语义相邻概念，在ERR指标上比SOTA基线平均提升12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉概念遗忘（visual concept unlearning）是生成模型安全与隐私领域的重要任务，需要在移除特定概念的同时保持模型对其他内容的生成能力。现有方法在概念去除的鲁棒性和相邻概念保留之间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: 在MapRoute框架基础上进行了三点改进：1）引入任务特定的训练目标以增强遗忘效果；2）构建更丰富的概念表示以捕获更全面的概念语义信息；3）采用语义路由机制，针对不同概念选择特定的映射器（mapper），实现更精准和灵活的概念去除。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4上使用Erasing-Retention-Robustness（ERR）评估指标，在五个概念类别上的平均性能比当前最先进基线提升12.1%，取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 该方法的有效性表明，结合任务特定训练目标和语义路由机制能够显著提升视觉概念遗忘的性能，在概念移除的鲁棒性与保留其他概念之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: We present our submission to Task 3 of the Gen$μ$ 2.0 Challenge on visual concept unlearning. Building on MapRoute, we introduce task-specific training objectives, richer concept representations, and semantic routing for concept-specific mapper selection. Our approach improves robust concept removal while preserving unrelated and semantically adjacent concepts. On the official benchmark, evaluated using the Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR) metric on Stable Diffusion v1.4, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 12.1\% on average across the five concept categories, achieving substantial gains.

</details>


### [83] [DreamX-Phi 1.0: Action-Conditioned Video World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13489)
*DreamX Team,Rui Chen,Xiangxiang Chu,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qingfeng Shi,Datao Tang,Jing Tang,Jun Wang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX-Phi 1.0是一个用于机器人操作的动作条件视频世界模型，通过几何编码、深度分支和对象一致性维护等技术，在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中取得了Track 1第一名和Track 2第二名的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型生成的预测虽然看起来逼真，但不一定忠于实际物理和操作逻辑，例如可能移动错误的机械臂或丢失被操作对象。因此需要确保预测既逼真又忠实于指令和动作序列。

Method: 该方法包含：(1) 通过PRoPE风格的几何编码将每只机械臂的SE(3)变换注入注意力机制，保持臂身份和刚体运动结构；(2) 轻量级深度分支用于场景级几何建模；(3) 使用SAM3掩码和冻结的V-JEPA教师模型维持被抓取对象的跨帧一致性；(4) 通过分布匹配蒸馏将多步生成器压缩为少步学生模型以提升部署效率。

Result: 在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中，DreamX-Phi 1.0在Track 1上获得第一名，在Track 2上获得第二名。

Conclusion: 将几何先验（SE(3)编码、深度信息）和对象一致性机制（SAM3+V-JEPA）融入视频世界模型，能够同时实现预测的真实感与任务忠实度，且通过蒸馏可以在保持性能的同时大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: We present \textbf{DreamX-Phi 1.0}, an action-conditioned video world model for robotic manipulation that, given an observed frame, a language instruction, and a prescribed action sequence comprising end-effector poses and gripper states, predicts the resulting future observations. Yet realism alone does not guarantee faithfulness: a convincing rollout can still move the wrong arm or lose the manipulated object. To ensure the prediction respects each arm's commanded path, we inject per-arm $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations into attention via \textbf{PRoPE-style geometric encoding}, preserving arm identity and rigid-motion structure. Action control alone does not fully constrain scene geometry or the evolution of small manipulated objects. We therefore add a lightweight \textbf{depth branch} for scene-level geometry and use \textbf{SAM3 masks} with a frozen \textbf{V-JEPA teacher} to maintain object consistency throughout grasping. We further distill the multi-step generator into a few-step student via distribution-matching distillation for efficient deployment. At the time of writing, \model{} achieves first place on Track~1 and second place on Track~2 of the WorldArena~2.0 Challenge. Our model and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [84] [TraVEL: Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning for Driving-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13495)
*Yi-Chung Chen,Philip Jacobson,Tom Lampo,Yiren Lu,Jin Yao,David I. Inouye,Jing Gao,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TraVEL框架，通过轨迹引导的视频嵌入学习微调通用多模态嵌入模型（Qwen3-VL-Embedding），以提升驾驶视频检索中运动相关事件（如转向、加速）的检索能力，在nuReasoning基准上将纵向和横向mAP分别提升9.8和4.7个点（2B模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模驾驶日志中的相关片段检索对数据管理、模型开发和安全性分析至关重要。现有的结构化检索系统依赖专家规则和复杂感知流水线，而通用多模态嵌入模型虽高效，但常依赖静态场景上下文捷径，难以区分运动中心事件（如左转vs右转、加速vs减速）。

Method: 首先在nuReasoning数据集上使用InfoNCE目标微调Qwen3-VL-Embedding，结合片段与推理轨迹进行训练；随后引入TraVEL框架，利用基于自车轨迹相似度的奖励信号，通过Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行运动感知微调。轨迹仅作为训练阶段的特权监督信息，推理时仍使用单向量视频嵌入，无需自车姿态或额外感知输出。

Result: TraVEL在多个模型规模上均提升了运动中心检索性能：相对SFT，2B模型纵向和横向mAP分别提升9.8和4.7个点，8B模型分别提升7.2和1.5个点。

Conclusion: TraVEL成功地将物理层面有依据的轨迹监督与高效的基于嵌入的检索相结合，在不引入推理时额外负担的前提下，显著提升了驾驶视频中运动事件的检索精度。

Abstract: Efficiently retrieving relevant clips from large-scale driving logs is essential for data curation, model development, and safety analysis. Structured and rule-based retrieval systems can explicitly target driving events, but typically require expert-defined rules, auxiliary data, and multi-stage perception pipelines. Multimodal embedding models offer a simpler and more efficient alternative by representing each video with a single searchable vector. However, general-purpose models often rely on shortcuts from static scene context and struggle to distinguish motion-centric events, such as turning left versus right or accelerating versus decelerating. In this work, we study how to adapt a general-purpose multimodal embedding model to driving-video retrieval. We first fine-tune Qwen3-VL-Embedding on paired clips and reasoning traces from nuReasoning using an InfoNCE objective. While this stage substantially improves overall retrieval, caption supervision alone remains insufficient for fine-grained motion understanding. We therefore introduce TraVEL (Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning), a motion-aware fine-tuning framework that uses ego-trajectory similarity as a reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. Trajectories serve only as privileged training supervision; retrieval still operates on single-vector video embeddings without ego poses, expert rules, or auxiliary perception outputs. We further construct a driving-video retrieval benchmark from nuReasoning. Experiments show that TraVEL improves motion-centric retrieval across model scales: relative to SFT, it raises longitudinal and lateral mAP by 9.8 and 4.7 points at 2B, with corresponding gains of 7.2 and 1.5 points at 8B. TraVEL thus combines physically grounded supervision with efficient embedding-based search.

</details>


### [85] [GS$^{2}$CI: Robust Gaussian Splatting For Snapshot Compressive Imaging via Large Vision Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13502)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Ping Wang,Xin Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从单次快照压缩成像(SCI)测量中重建高质量3D场景的新框架，该框架结合3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)和视觉基础模型(VFM)的先验知识，并引入SCI特定的致密化策略(OSGR)来解决SCI监督模糊导致的优化不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SCI的3D场景重建方法面临信息丢失、视点多样性有限以及联合优化3D表示与相机位姿计算负担大等挑战，需要一种能够在单次SCI测量中高效重建高质量3D场景的方法。

Method: 该方法包含三个关键组件：(1)主重建阶段，结合测量导出的3D VFM初始化与SCI感知的高斯优化；(2)粗阶段收敛后，利用辅助2D VFM在合成视点提供伪视图监督进行局部外观细化；(3)引入OSGR策略，通过局部不透明度统计增强分割候选、均值不透明度正则防止损失补偿式不透明度膨胀、以及显式候选比例和高斯数量约束来限制表示增长。

Result: 在多个基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法取得了最强的整体性能，在重建质量和视点变化鲁棒性方面领先，同时具有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了结合3DGS与大规模VFM先验可以有效地从单次SCI测量中重建高质量3D场景，并提出了一个专门针对SCI模糊监督问题的致密化策略来提升重建稳定性。

Abstract: Snapshot Compressive Imaging (SCI) offers an efficient solution for high-speed video acquisition and, under exposure-time camera--scene relative motion, multi-view scene capture by compressing temporal or spatial information into a single 2D measurement. While recent studies have explored SCI for 3D scene reconstruction, existing methods struggle with significant challenges due to information loss, limited viewpoint diversity, and the computational burden of jointly optimizing 3D representations and camera poses. In this work, we propose a novel framework that reconstructs high-quality 3D scenes from a single SCI measurement by leveraging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and the powerful priors of large-scale vision foundation models (VFMs). Our primary reconstruction combines measurement-derived 3D VFM initialization with SCI-aware Gaussian optimization. After coarse-stage convergence, an auxiliary 2D VFM provides pseudo-view supervision at synthesized viewpoints for local appearance refinement. To further address the instability caused by ambiguous SCI supervision during 3DGS optimization, we introduce Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR), an SCI-specific densification strategy that augments split candidates using local opacity statistics, discourages loss-compensating opacity inflation through mean-opacity regulation, and bounds representation growth with explicit candidate-ratio and Gaussian-count constraints. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves the strongest overall performance, combining leading reconstruction quality and robustness to viewpoint variation with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [TabSOM: A tabular-to-image encoding method based on self-organizing maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13513)
*David Chushig-Muzo,María Ángeles Rodríguez de Cara,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,Luis Zhinin-Vera,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TabSOM是一种基于自组织映射（SOM）的表格数据转图像编码方法，通过匈牙利算法将每个特征分配到固定画布位置，并利用SOM组件平面构建特征关系图。图像由两个多尺度节点通道组成：一个编码特征值，另一个编码特征间交互。在十二种已有方法对比中，TabSOM在全部数据集上排名第一或第二，且方差最低，同时提供了两种可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格转图像方法虽然能利用CNN和ViT的高预测性能，但仅编码了每个特征的边际值，丢失了特征之间的相互关系信息，导致性能与可解释性受限。

Method: TabSOM基于SOM构建：通过碰撞避免的匈牙利赋值将每个特征固定在画布位置，从SOM组件平面推导特征关系图。图像堆叠两个多尺度节点通道——一个以固定尺度编码特征值，另一个将成对特征交互编码为相关特征间的空间连接。同时提出两种基于SOM的可解释性方法：原型启发的部分依赖图和类别分离重要性得分。

Result: 在公开二分类数据集上与十二种已有表格转图像方法对比，TabSOM在每个数据集上排名第一或第二，且在所有评估方法中方差最低。可解释性结果与随机森林、XGBoost和SHAP对比验证，类别分离得分在top特征上与基线合理一致，同时捕获了来自输入数据的互补结构信息。

Conclusion: TabSOM为将深度学习架构应用于表格数据提供了有效且可解释的途径，弥合了该领域中性能与可解释性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods have emerged as novel approaches to leverage the high predictive performance of convolutional neural networks and vision transformers. They convert tabular data into image representations, mapping each feature at a fixed pixel location derived from a dimensionality-reduction method (e.g., t-SNE, UMAP, PCA). However, they encode only the marginal value of each feature and discard information about feature relationships. We propose TabSOM, a tabular-to-image encoding built on the Self-Organizing Map (SOM), which provides: (i) a spatial layout in which every input feature occupies a fixed canvas position derived from its component plane via collision-free Hungarian assignment; and (ii) a graph that captures pairwise feature relationships derived from the SOM component planes. The resulting image stacks two multi-scale node channels: one encodes feature values at fixed scales, while the other encodes pairwise feature interactions as spatial connections between related features. Two SOM-derived interpretability approaches are introduced: a prototype-inspired partial dependence plot and a class--separation importance score. Benchmarked against twelve existing tabular-to-image methods across public binary-classification datasets, TabSOM ranks first or second on every dataset and achieves the lowest variance of any method evaluated. Interpretability obtained with TabSOM was validated against Random Forest, XGBoost, and SHAP, the class-separation score shows reasonable agreement with established baselines on the top-ranked features while capturing complementary structural information from input data. These results demonstrate that TabSOM provides an effective and interpretable approach for applying deep learning architectures to tabular data, bridging the performance--interpretability gap in this domain.

</details>


### [87] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCULPT通过减法合成的方式（subtractive composition）实现部件感知的3D生成，在结构化3D潜在空间中迭代地联合预测"一个提取部件+剩余物体"，并通过耦合去噪过程确保部件与剩余部分无缝衔接，无需后期拼接修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部件感知3D生成方法存在两类问题：基于分割的方法在生成完成后才确定部件边界，结构固定且不可编辑；基于加法合成的方法虽然暴露了部件数量，但共享边界容易出现间隙、相互穿透和材质不连续等问题。作者希望通过减法合成方式同时兼顾完整物体几何质量与结构性部件分解。

Method: SCULPT框架采用减法合成策略：(1) 在结构化3D潜在空间中表示完整物体；(2) 迭代使用联合分割预测器（joint split predictor）生成一个提取部件和剩余物体；(3) 预测器基于图像和当前3D状态执行耦合去噪过程，部件与剩余部分同步生成而非事后调和；(4) 在两者原生稀疏3D支撑的并集上处理输出，允许相邻支撑重叠而非强制不相交体素分区；(5) 通过rollout终止条件（支撑为空或达到安全上限）自适应确定部件数量。

Result: 在PartObjaverse数据集上取得了最先进的几何效果，同时保持部件组装后的完整物体重建质量。在四张数据集图像、一张文本到图像生成输入和一张真实世界照片上的结果展示了超出基准的细粒度纹理部件分解能力。

Conclusion: SCULPT通过减法合成框架有效解决了现有部件感知3D生成中"整体质量"与"部件结构"之间的矛盾，证明了耦合去噪和支撑重叠策略能够实现高质量的部件自适应分解。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [88] [Alaya-EVOKE: From Linear-Scaling Supervision to Endless World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13546)
*Yuanyang Yin,Gongxuan Wang,Yifan Zhan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evoke通过外部化的相机索引世界状态银行和重新设计的长期教师模型，实现了有界上下文下的开放式交互式世界模型生成，支持持久记忆、响应式交互和长时间视野生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要同时满足持久记忆、响应式交互和长时间生成三个要求，但传统方法在去噪器上下文或键值缓存中维护历史会导致成本不断增长，在会话长度与记忆保留之间存在矛盾；同时少步生成能力受限于教师模型。

Method: Evoke将场景几何外部化为相机索引的世界状态银行，仅检索与视图相关的信息以保持去噪器上下文有界；重新设计教师模型用于长时间视野监督，采用分块分组、选择性远帧检索和线性注意力全局状态的稀疏注意力，实现线性的内存和计算增长；通过30秒分布匹配目标在自强制rollout下训练，将能力迁移到无需分类器引导的三步学生模型。

Result: 在单个H200上以384×640分辨率，每个1.5秒片段生成仅需2.11秒；作为三步世界模型，在WBench上达到最先进性能，在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Evoke通过有界上下文和循环外部记忆支持开放式的持续演进生成，有效解决了长期漂移问题，同时保持了响应式条件控制能力。

Abstract: Interactive world models must support persistent memory, responsive interaction, and long-horizon generation, yet these requirements place conflicting demands on the model. Maintaining history in the denoiser context or key-value cache incurs growing cost, forcing a trade-off between session length and retained memory, while low-latency interaction relies on few-step generation whose capabilities are bounded by its teacher. Evoke addresses both limitations by externalizing persistent world state and redesigning the teacher for long-horizon interactive generation. Scene geometry is maintained in an external, camera-indexed world state bank, from which only view-relevant information is retrieved, keeping the denoiser context bounded as the session grows. Rather than treating the teacher as a fixed generator, we design it for long-horizon supervision: its sparse attention combines chunk-wise grouping, retrieval of selected distant frames, and a linear-attention global state, yielding linear growth in memory and compute while enabling supervision over long horizons. Such supervision exposes content drift that stays locally plausible within short windows, while per-chunk conditioning enables prompt changes and event control throughout the sequence. A 30-second distribution-matching objective, applied under self-forced rollouts, transfers both capabilities to a three-step student that uses no classifier-free guidance, improving resistance to long-term drift while preserving responsive conditioning. With bounded context and recurrent external memory, Evoke supports open-ended, continuously evolving generation; on a single H200 at $384\times 640$, each $1.5\,\mathrm{s}$ chunk is generated in $2.11\,\mathrm{s}$. As a three-step world model, Evoke achieves state-of-the-art performance on WBench while remaining competitive on VBench-Long and VBench-2.0.

</details>


### [89] [PlayWorld: Benchmarking World Models with Agent Players over Long-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13552)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhiyuan Xu,Yiyang Wang,Yuxiang Lu,Junyi Li,Shuyang Chen,Yuan Gao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PlayWorld基准，采用多模态Agent Players与视频世界模型交互，以长时程目标为导向评估模型，涵盖171个场景和四个核心维度，实验发现现有世界模型在长时程交互目标上仍不可靠，尤其在空间一致性和状态持续演化方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型评估方法主要依赖固定动作序列进行条件生成，但不同模型为达成相同目标所需的动作序列差异很大，导致固定动作条件评估无法公平地进行跨模型比较，需要一种以目标为导向的交互式评估范式。

Method: 采用多模态Agent Players与视频世界模型进行交互，围绕指定的长时程目标完成任务；构建PlayWorld基准包含171个场景，每个场景配有明确目标；从几何一致性、交互保真度、视野外演化、洞察演化四个核心维度以及视频质量和可控性两个基础能力维度进行评估。

Result: 对九个最先进的世界模型进行实验，结果表明现有模型在长时程交互目标上仍不可靠，特别是在维持空间一致性和持久状态演化方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前视频世界模型在长时程交互式目标下仍存在显著缺陷，需要进一步改进空间一致性和状态持久演化能力；PlayWorld基准与代码已开源，为后续研究提供评估平台。

Abstract: Video world models simulate future states conditioned on current observations and user actions. Recent systems have demonstrated impressive video consistency and action controllability over long sequences. However, fairly comparing these interactive models remains challenging. In practice, a human player typically evaluates a world model by pursuing long-horizon objectives through interaction. For example, a user may turn around 360 degrees to see whether the environment remains consistent, or walk into the water and inspect whether realistic water ripples are generated. The action sequence required to achieve the same objective may vary substantially between models, making fixed action-conditioned evaluation unsuitable for cross-model comparison. To address this, we employ multi-modal Agent Players to interact with world models toward specified long-horizon objectives. Building on this paradigm, we introduce PlayWorld, a benchmark providing 171 scenarios, each with a specified objective. To evaluate performance thoroughly, we assess models along four core dimensions: geometry consistency, interaction fidelity, out-of-sight evolution, and insight evolution. In addition, we incorporate basic ability metrics for video quality and controllability. Experiments across nine state-of-the-art world models reveal that current models remain unreliable on long-horizon interactive objectives, particularly in maintaining spatial consistency and persistent state evolution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kxding/PlayWorld.

</details>


### [90] [V-RAE: Rethinking Video Latent Spaces for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13556)
*Minghui Guo,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-RAE，一种构建在冻结视觉基础模型表示之上的视频表示自编码器，通过轻量级时间池化模块去除时间冗余并保留语义结构，在视频重建、语义探测和类条件生成任务上均取得优于现有视频VAE的性能，并引入tFVD作为更可靠的时间一致性诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自编码器的潜在空间主要针对像素级重建进行优化，缺乏高层语义组织，而重建最优的潜在空间并不一定适合生成建模，因此需要一种能同时兼顾重建质量与语义结构、适合生成任务的视频表示学习方法。

Method: V-RAE利用冻结的视觉基础模型（如四种代表性编码器）提取语义特征，通过轻量级时间池化模块去除时间冗余、保留语义结构，再由视频解码器从压缩特征中重建连续运动，从而构建紧致的生成式潜在空间。

Result: V-RAE在K600上达到2.13 rFVD，优于所有被评估的大规模预训练视频VAE；保留的语义信息远超传统视频分词器潜码；在匹配生成设置下，UCF101和K600分别达到117.86和19.16的gFVD，收敛速度提升最高6倍；在Cityscapes未来预测任务上也优于Wan 2.2 VAE潜在空间。

Conclusion: 实验证明冻结的语义表示可以同时支持视频重建、生成和预测建模，且重建质量本身不足以表征生成效用，tFVD作为时间一致性诊断指标与下游生成质量具有更可靠的相关性。

Abstract: Latent video generation relies on autoencoders to define a compact space in which generative models operate. Although video autoencoder architectures have evolved substantially, their latent spaces are still optimized primarily for pixel-level reconstruction and provide limited high-level semantic organization. A reconstruction-optimal latent space, however, need not be well suited to generative modeling. We propose V-RAE, a video representation autoencoder that builds compact generative latents on top of frozen vision foundation model representations. A lightweight temporal pooling module removes temporal redundancy while preserving semantic structure, and a video decoder reconstructs continuous motion from the compressed features. We evaluate V-RAE with four representative frozen encoders on video reconstruction, semantic probing, and class-conditional generation. V-RAE achieves 2.13 rFVD on K600, outperforming all evaluated large-scale pretrained video VAEs. Its latents retain substantially more semantic information than conventional video tokenizer latents. Under matched generation settings, our best variant achieves gFVD scores of 117.86 and 19.16 on UCF101 and K600, respectively, while converging up to 6x faster}. We further show that reconstruction quality alone is insufficient to characterize generative utility and introduce tFVD, a temporal-coherence diagnostic that correlates more reliably with downstream generation quality. Beyond video generation, V-RAE also improves future video prediction on Cityscapes over the Wan 2.2 VAE latent space under matched prediction settings. Taken together, the experiments show that frozen semantic representations can support video reconstruction, generation, and predictive modeling. The project page: https://v-rae.github.io/.

</details>


### [91] [AutoDesign: Meta-Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)
*Yaxin Luo,Haobin Jiang,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jing Li,Xiaofu Chen,Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Jiacheng Cui,Zhiqiang Shen,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoDesign框架，将多模态源到结构化媒体输出的转换视为长期智能体过程。通过元Harness优化器引导代码智能体基于rollout反馈递归改进harness系统，在学术论文转海报任务中，AutoDesign在PosterBench上以78.32分超越商业系统Claude Design 7.45分，并在人类评估中获得最高偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness系统是静态的，无法与人类设计先验对齐，也不能通过经验积累实现递归自我改进。理想的harness系统应积累可复用经验并驱动递归性自我提升，但目前缺乏这样的能力。

Method: 提出AutoDesign框架：元harness优化器引导代码智能体基于rollout反馈递归改进harness系统。构建PosterBench基准（100篇论文主轨跨五个学科）和PosterBench-mini（10篇论文子集）用于受控评估，聚焦学术论文到海报生成任务。

Result: 在PosterBench主轨取得最高分78.32，超越Claude Design 7.45分。在七个受控配置中集成学习到的DesignHarness使平均分数从54.99提升至67.39（+12.4%）。完全自主长时间循环中执行253次工具调用和11次编辑回合，40分钟内花费不到3美元，达到平均会议海报质量。系统盲人研究显示AutoDesign获得最高人类偏好。

Conclusion: AutoDesign验证了通过元优化器驱动harness系统递归自我改进的可行性，以低成本高效超越商业系统，为多模态媒体生成提供了一个新范式。

Abstract: Transforming multimodal sources into condensed and structured media outputs can be fundamentally conceptualized as a long-horizon agentic process centered on a model-harness system. While an ideal harness system should align with human design priors and accumulate reusable experience through empirical exploration to drive recursive self-improvement, existing paradigms remain static and fall short of this capability. In this paper, we present AutoDesign, a framework that aligns with human design priors, where a meta-harness optimizer guides a code agent to recursively improve harness based on rollout feedback. To instantiate and evaluate this framework, we focus on the academic paper-to-poster generation task and introduce PosterBench, comprising a 100-paper Main Track spanning five disciplines and PosterBench-mini, a shared 10-paper subset for controlled evaluation. On the PosterBench Main Track, AutoDesign achieves the highest score of 78.32, surpassing the closed-source commercial system Claude Design by 7.45 points. Across seven controlled code-agent-model configurations, integrating the learned DesignHarness consistently improves performance, increasing the average PosterBench Score from 54.99 to 67.39 (+12.4%). In a fully autonomous long-horizon loop, it executes 253 tool calls and 11 editing turns within 40 minutes for under $3, reaching average conference-poster quality in human evaluation. A system-blind human study further demonstrates that AutoDesign achieves the highest human preference among evaluated systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoKiFormer，一种新型大语言模型架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别增强序列内部局部信息建模和序列外部全局知识存储，预训练收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构存在两个关键局限：自注意力缺乏局部性归纳偏置，导致对序列内局部信息的冗余建模；混合专家（MoE）将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外全局知识的灵活访问。这两点限制了预训练效率。

Method: LoKiFormer在标准解码器上增加两个模块：1）局部融合注意力（LFA），在注意力机制中引入卷积融合，显式捕捉局部模式，使注意力在更具信息量的表示上运行；2）知识记忆模块（KMM），引入参数化键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，实现存储与计算的解耦，支持直接知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练中收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，验证了该架构在信息整合效率和有效性上的优势。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer通过LFA和KMM分别解决了局部信息冗余建模和全局知识存储耦合的问题，实现了更高效的预训练，展现了优于现有LLM架构的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [93] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与卫星天气）构建了尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区冰湖溃决、滑坡及小洪水等三类灾害的预测模型。结果表明：前期天气条件对触发时机的预测具有较高准确率（ROC 0.73-0.83），而地形对易发性的排名作用有限（经严格交叉验证后为0.54-0.76）；深度学习模型未能显著超越梯度提升基线。研究最终提供尼泊尔优先关注清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有可行性研究发现：变形数据可指示冰碛坝的稳定性（哪个湖在失稳），天气数据可指示风险窗口（何时面临压力），但缺乏将这些信息整合为预测模型的方案。本文旨在填补这一空白，利用免费数据构建可预测"哪个地点易发"和"何种触发条件何时到达"的统一模型。

Method: 基于589个冰湖溃决事件（HMAGLOFDB数据库）和数千个编目滑坡事件，将每个事件与相似但未失稳的对照点进行匹配。采用空间交叉验证（按整幅地图瓦片留出训练数据）防止空间自相关导致虚高评分。使用前期气象条件预测触发时机，用地形特征预测易发程度，并对比五种深度学习模型与梯度提升基线模型，另构建三规则决策树作为可解释基准。

Result: 前期天气对触发时机的预测：大溃决ROC 0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82。地形简单评分看似接近0.9但纠偏后为0.76（大溃决）、0.71（滑坡）、0.54（小洪水，不优于随机）。溃决信号在尼泊尔单一区域内可达0.89。深度学习模型未显著超越梯度提升基线；对湖库灾害基线模型以三规则决策树（崎岖度+季风降雨）即可复现获胜结果。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据可在触发时机预测上提供可靠信号，而纯地形指标对易发性的排序能力有限（尤其对小洪水类风险）。模型输出应作为优先排序参考而非确定性预测，同时需明确免费数据的能力边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [94] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History），一种通过周期性缓存循环状态检查点（状态锚点）并结合内容条件锚键进行注意力聚合的网络架构，在保持高效计算的同时扩展状态空间模型的记忆容量，在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上优于多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽然具有随上下文长度增长的分词级记忆，但训练时计算复杂度呈二次方，推理时KV缓存线性增长；而循环替代方案虽然解码高效，但固定大小的状态会被后续更新覆盖，导致早期信息丢失，在召回密集型任务上表现不佳。

Method: MARCH周期性缓存累积循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，每个锚点关联紧凑的内容条件锚键。模型维护一个可随上下文长度增长的记忆库，在每步生成锚点查询来关注所有因果可用的状态锚点，输出通过注意力风格聚合所有历史锚点与当前状态计算结果。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上一致优于多种线性注意力变体，表明内容路由的状态缓存能显著增强循环长程记忆能力。

Conclusion: MARCH通过内容条件状态缓存机制在长上下文记忆中实现了历史分辨率与记忆成本之间的可控权衡，同时保持了循环架构固有的高效计算路径，验证了扩展状态空间模型固定大小维度的可行性。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [95] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VGG-MADiffRL（值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法）和MDCA（基于扩散的分层控制架构），用于解决多AUV自组网在受限水声通信、动态拓扑和海洋扰动下的协同目标跟踪问题，通过三层闭环控制框架和值梯度引导的扩散策略生成，实现更快收敛、更高跟踪精度和更稳定的训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的协同目标跟踪方法存在高维联合状态-动作建模复杂、策略生成对噪声敏感等问题，导致训练不稳定、跟踪性能退化，亟需一种能够适应动态水下环境、稳定收敛的协同跟踪方法。

Method: 1) 将多AUV自组网协同跟踪建模为MDP，建模声纳探测机制和洋流扰动；2) 提出MDCA三层闭环控制框架：全局智能控制层、局部在线训练层、物理动作执行层；3) 在局部在线训练层中，VGG-MADiffRL基于扩散策略，在反向去噪过程中引入值梯度引导动作生成，向更高期望回报方向优化；4) 采用孪生值网络、联合优化和软目标更新机制，缓解过估计和训练振荡。

Result: 实验结果表明，VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中取得了更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和工程实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法通过值梯度引导的扩散策略和分层控制架构，有效解决了多AUV自组网协同跟踪中的训练不稳定和策略生成噪声敏感问题，为复杂水下环境下的多智能体协同控制提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [96] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成建模统一表述为路径积分形式，其中流模型、扩散模型、变分模型和对抗模型均可视为同一主作用量下的不同评估原则。该框架的MSRJD形式将自由与相互作用的概率流分离，并引入图论微扰理论，提供了一阶环路修正，无需额外随机采样成本即可将确定性采样器误差从53%降至1.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（流模型、扩散模型、变分模型、对抗模型）各自具有独立的形式体系，缺乏统一的数学框架来理解它们之间的关系和差异。同时，确定性采样器在求解概率流时存在较大偏差，而精确的随机采样式方法通常成本高昂。作者希望建立统一框架以揭示不同生成模型间的内在联系，并为改进采样精度提供理论工具。

Method: 论文将生成建模重新表述为路径积分（主作用量）形式，并证明流模型、扩散模型、变分模型和对抗模型是对同一主作用量取不同评估原则的结果。通过MSRJD形式化将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，使得系统可应用图示微扰理论。在具体操作上：(1) 展开得到一阶环路修正，用于改进确定性采样器；(2) 将不完美的学习评分函数作为插入项处理，推导出响应加权的评分匹配目标；(3) 将对称等变漂移设计转化为算符展开，并赋予EFT（有效场论）幂次计数。

Result: 在可解和非线性漂移系统上的实验验证中，该一阶环路修正将确定性采样器的树级误差从53%降低至1.6%，显著提升了采样精度且未增加随机采样成本。同时证明了该框架能够统一处理不同生成模型的评估原则，并给出有理论依据的评分匹配目标改进和对称性设计方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成建模提供了一个统一的理论框架，不仅揭示了流模型、扩散模型、变分模型和对抗模型之间的内在联系，还通过微扰展开提供了实用的精度提升方案。这一框架有望为后续生成模型的统一理论构建和更高效的采样算法设计奠定基础。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [97] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了X-AddGraph，一个针对动态图异常检测模型AddGraph的严格事后可解释性框架。该框架基于双重时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个与模型架构模块对齐的组件：基于梯度的空间相关性归因、零成本读取推理期间已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间归因）、以及通过循环隐藏状态的反向梯度回滚（长期时间归因）。由于检测器被冻结，检测性能完全保持不变（ΔAUC=0）。在UCI Message基准上，X-AddGraph在保持所有分数不变的同时为每条边提供了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图中的深度学习异常检测器虽然准确率高，但完全不透明——当一条边被标记为异常时，分析人员只能收到一个分数而得不到任何理由。在需要可审计和可信自动化决策的协作式、受监管的信息系统中，这种不透明性是不可接受的。AddGraph作为动态图边级异常检测的基础性GCN+GRU框架，此前从未配备任何形式的可解释性机制。

Method: 提出严格事后可解释性框架X-AddGraph，基于双重时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个组件：1）基于梯度的相关性归因（空间）；2）直接读取推理期间已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间，零额外成本）；3）通过循环隐藏状态的梯度回滚（长期时间）。由于检测器完全冻结，检测性能精确保留。

Result: 检测性能精确保持（ΔAUC=0，经实证验证到小数点后十位）。在UCI Message基准上，训练好的AddGraph基线达到平均每快照AUC 0.8705，超过原始发表结果。在四种边群体（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）的评估中，长期归因识别的历史快照携带的对抗性信号显著多于随机选择（0.127 vs 0.074），这是任何空间盲解释器都无法提供的能力。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph在不牺牲任何检测性能的前提下，为AddGraph框架提供了全面、可审计的边级异常解释能力，填补了动态图异常检测可解释性的空白。代码已开源以供完整复现。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [98] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的睡眠分期参考图谱（LBH）构建方法，利用多评分者数据通过混淆矩阵建模各评分者的分期行为，聚合多个专家的标签以生成更可靠的参考标准，在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上相比数据集图谱和最佳评分者图谱均取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动睡眠分期研究以单一参考图谱作为评估标准，但已知不同评分者之间存在标注变异性（inter-scorer variability），单一参考可能不够可靠。本研究旨在利用多评分数据集，从多个专家的集体行为中构建更可靠的参考标签，以提升分期评估的准确性。

Method: 使用公开的DOD-H和DOD-O数据集，提取EEG（C3-M2）和下巴EMG信号的30秒epoch，每模态提取30个特征（共60个特征）。提出学习型参考图谱（LBH），通过机器学习模型推导各评分者的混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估计每个真实睡眠分期在给定评分者标签下的概率，跨评分者聚合概率分配最终标签。使用随机森林、支持向量机和多层感知机三种分类器，对比仅EEG和EEG+EMG两种设置，与数据集图谱（DH）和最佳评分者图谱（BSH）进行比较。

Result: LBH在所有设置下均一致提升了整体性能。最佳结果由随机森林结合EEG+EMG取得：DOD-H上准确率86.07%、精确率85.46%、F1分数85.29%；DOD-O上准确率86.04%、精确率85.21%、F1分数84.70%。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模能够在保留每位专家信息的同时改善参考图谱的构建质量，为自动睡眠分期评估提供了更可靠的参照基准。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [99] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer模型在残差流中存在一种特殊的"预测方向"（prediction direction），即当前预测token的反嵌入方向，它作为内容定义的优先锚点，将残差流几何和行为上按与预测的邻近程度分层。该分层在18种模型中普遍存在，预测近端区域高度结构化并与相关提示聚类，远端区域较平坦并反区分提示组。扰动最近的方差方向会导致立即发散和任务框架转换，而扰动次近方向仅延迟发散，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已发现Transformer模型会发展出"特权基"（privileged bases），即统计特征区别于残差流其余部分的坐标轴。但尚不清楚这种基选择的是什么方向。理解预测方向作为特权锚点的作用，有助于解释高维计算如何与线性读出共存这一根本问题。

Method: 作者系统地研究了18种模型（包括稠密和混合专家模型，参数量7B-120B，基座版和指令微调版），以预测token的反嵌入方向为锚点，测量残差流相对于该方向的几何和因果结构。通过方差分析、几何梯度和因果扰动实验，揭示预测近端与远端区域的结构差异。

Result: 预测方向作为内容定义的优先锚点在所有18种模型中成立。预测接口是窄而尺度不变的，集中了读出相关结构，而预测远端部分随模型规模扩大。预测方向几乎与主方差轴正交，导致基于方差的分析只能部分恢复这种组织。因果实验表明，干扰最接近预测的方差方向导致立即发散和频繁的任务框架转换，而干扰次近方向仅延迟发散但保持框架；远端方向虽逐方向弱对齐但因果和时间上承重。

Conclusion: 预测方向是一种区别于先前描述的坐标轴的独特特权锚点。该发现为高维计算与线性读出如何共存提供了几何解释：模型将读出关键信息集中在预测近端的窄界面中，而利用预测远端的大容量空间进行柔性和可扩展的计算。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [100] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习表示与摘要选择的客观分类方法，用于组织不同飞行姿态下阵风引起的载荷复杂性。在包含3480个压力载荷测量数据的飞翼模型数据库上，该方法识别出9种跨姿态重复出现的基本响应类型，并保持了基于粗参数标注的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否存在一种客观的分类标准，能够组织多种飞行条件下阵风诱发载荷的复杂性，同时保持与基于飞行姿态等粗参数标注同等程度的可解释性。

Method: 通过机器学习表示编码大量实验观测数据，并应用摘要程序选择最小的高显著性样本子集。这些样本提供基于相似性的客观分类标准，便于专家检查并可用于更精细的实验。

Result: 在包含3480个随机阵风诱导压力载荷测量（跨越六种飞行姿态）的飞翼模型数据库上，发现了9种跨多种姿态重复出现的基本响应类型；对类型瞬态响应的分析揭示了底层流体力学物理直觉。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功地在保持可解释性的前提下，为阵风载荷提供了客观的、基于样例的分类准则，有助于专家分析和后续更精细的实验研究。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [101] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以心搏骤停后神经系统预后预测为案例，提出了一种显式区分"确定病例"和"不确定病例"（因治疗决策导致临床结局不可观测、由专家提供反事实猜测）的预测模型评估框架，揭示了标准评估指标可能掩盖关键失败模式的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的临床预测模型假设患者结局可清晰观测，但治疗决策会使某些患者的临床相关结局永久不可观测。心跳骤停后神经预后预测正是此类问题——1,429名患者因治疗决策导致结局不确定，标准评估方法无法捕捉这些对预后支持最关键的患者的失败模式。

Method: 提出一个评估框架，将患者分为确定病例（观测到临床结局）和不确定病例（由独立临床专家提供反事实结局猜测），分别评估模型。同时提出一个简单预测模型，利用两类病例的标签，并显式允许在两类之间进行权衡（trade-off）。使用真实数据集（2,497名患者）评估神经网络模型和若干表格基线模型。

Result: 模型即使在确定病例上具有相似的AUROC，其在确定病例上的Brier分数和不确定病例的概率估计上却可能存在显著差异。提升与不确定病例标签的一致性通常以牺牲确定病例的准确性为代价，揭示了标准评估所隐藏的显式权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定临床结局是否可观测时，传统评估指标可能遗漏对预后支持最关键的患者的失败模式。将评估显式拆分到确定和不确定病例，能更好地揭示模型在真实临床应用中的表现差距。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [102] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对一个组织在预算约束下选择最优目标人群的离线策略评估问题进行了系统基准测试。研究对比了六种估计器在五个数据集上的表现，发现三个关键问题：弱重叠主要由记录器与目标动作的对齐决定而非记录器锐度；交叉拟合结果扰动无法解决优化者诅咒；倾向得分估计误差是最大的性能退化来源。文章还指出常用有效样本量指标在单个日志内部排序能力弱，且其截断点不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织需要在部署确定性top-k目标规则之前，知道该规则在历史日志数据上能获得多少收益。离线策略评估承诺可以从日志数据中实现这一目标，但由于top-k策略是确定性策略，消除了所有动作上的平均化，导致弱重叠问题直接冲击估计结果。现有研究缺乏对这些机制的系统验证。

Method: 作者对比了六种估计器，使用五个数据集和两种已知效应扫描进行基准测试，并用非模拟配对参考验证机制。实验覆盖了弱重叠形成机制分析（记录器-目标动作对齐vs记录器锐度）、优化者诅咒的交叉拟合效果、倾向得分估计误差的影响，以及有效样本量作为风险排名指标的有效性检验。

Result: 主要发现：（1）弱重叠由记录器对目标动作的采样概率决定，而非记录器锐度；基于目标分数构建的记录器即使锐化也几乎不改变重叠，但动作级别的分歧会彻底瓦解重叠。（2）有效样本量能跨环境排序风险，但无法在单个日志内对候选方案排序，其截断点不可转移。（3）交叉拟合结果扰动无法修复优化者诅咒，反而可能使重用偏置更严重；诚实的策略级分割需要改变估计目标而非去偏。（4）倾向得分估计误差是最大的退化源：折叠外估计对IPS伤害最大，但几乎不影响双重稳健估计，甚至可能反转重叠诊断本身。

Conclusion: 弱重叠的发生机制与直觉相反——关键在于记录器与目标的动作对齐而非锐度；交叉拟合无法解决优化者诅咒需要改变估计目标；倾向得分误差是最需要关注的退化源。作者发布了基准测试框架，仅使用公开数据。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions.
