Abstract: Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance but incur substantial inference cost due to repeated high-dimensional matrix multiplications. We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference. Mechanistic ablations reveal a structural asymmetry within Transformers: attention-side computations are substantially more reducible than MLP components. Finally, wall-clock benchmarks with custom kernels on an NVIDIA A100 show that these computational savings can translate into practical runtime gains, especially at longer sequence lengths. Together, these results position RMM as a scalable direction for input-adaptive inference-time optimization.

</details>


### [171] [Symmetry-Breaking De Novo Crystal Generation via Markovian Jump Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13457)
*Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对称破缺晶体扩散模型（SbCD），利用马尔可夫跳变-扩散过程模拟物理中的自发对称破缺，从最低对称性先验出发反向生成完整的晶体结构规格，显式建模空间群之间的转变，在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型只能生成到位点对称性级别，且在生成过程中依赖从经验分布中采样空间群，无法捕捉全局对称性和结构依赖性，导致生成结果不完整。

Method: 受物理学中自发对称破缺启发，设计基于马尔可夫跳变-扩散过程的扩散框架，从最低对称性先验出发反向遍历不同空间群，显式建模空间群间的转变，从而生成完整的晶体结构规格。

Result: 在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上的从头生成实验中，SbCD相比对称保持的基线模型取得了大幅度的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过物理启发的对称破缺机制与跳变-扩散过程相结合，为晶体材料的生成建模提供了新的有效视角，展示了优异的生成性能。

Abstract: Generating crystals has recently attracted significant interest due to their broad applications in materials science. However, existing generative models struggle to produce complete crystallographic specifications, limiting their ability to capture global symmetry and structural dependencies. In particular, current state-of-the-art approaches generate crystals only up to site symmetries and rely on sampling space groups from empirical distributions during generation. Inspired by \emph{spontaneous symmetry breaking} in physics, where crystals break symmetries under external conditions, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework that generates full structure specifications by reversing from the lowest-symmetry priors. Our method leverages a Markovian jump-diffusion process to model these symmetry-breaking dynamics, enabling it to traverse different space groups in a physically motivated manner. Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [172] [Doubly Robust Estimation of Causal Effect on CVR with Targeted Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13461)
*Jiayi Dan,Bo Li,Lu Deng,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对链式结果变量（如点击后转化率CVR）的双重稳健因果效应估计器，并基于半参数理论设计了目标正则化框架，以解决现有方法中的样本选择偏差和方差增大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR是电商和广告中的关键指标，估计其因果效应很重要。但现有因果推断方法直接应用于点击样本会引入样本选择偏差和方差增大；已有的"理想损失"方法虽然实现了损失的无偏估计，但无法保证最终估计量的无偏性。

Method: 从半参数理论角度出发，开发了一个针对链式结构结果（如CVR）的双重稳健因果效应估计器，并详细推导了其理论性质。该估计器相比nuisance参数估计具有更快的收敛速度，对灵活的非参数估计器（包括神经网络）更稳健。基于理论发现，进一步设计了基于目标正则化的框架以提升数值稳定性和实际可用性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性和稳健性。同时发现，将损失去偏方法与标准因果估计器简单组合的效果不如所提方法，突显了针对CVR类目标开发具有坚实理论保证的新估计器的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双重稳健估计器在理论保证和实际性能上都优于简单组合方法，为解决CVR因果效应估计中的样本选择偏差问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a key metric in various scenarios including e-commerce and advertising, reflecting the efficiency and user experience in the second stage of the conversion process. Estimating the causal effect on CVR is therefore of great practical importance. However, directly applying existing causal inference methods to clicked samples introduces sample selection bias and increased variance due to the exclusion of non-click data. Recent studies on CVR prediction introduce "ideal loss", which optimizes model parameters using an unbiased estimate of the loss over the full sample. Nevertheless, there is no guarantee that unbiasedness of the loss implies unbiasedness of the final estimator.
  We revisit this challenge from the perspective of semiparametric theory. Specifically, we develop a new doubly robust causal effect estimator for chain-structured outcomes such as CVR, and derive its theoretical properties in detail. It achieves a faster convergence rate compared to nuisance parameters estimation and is therefore more robust when using flexible nonparametric estimators, including neural networks. Based on these theoretical findings, we further design a framework based on targeted regularization to improve numerical stability and practical applicability.
  Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method. In addition, we find that naively combining loss debiasing with standard causal estimators underperforms our method, highlighting the necessity of developing the new estimator tailored to this CVR-style objective with solid theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [173] [Concept Drift Detection and Adaptive Retraining of Malware Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13465)
*Christofer Washington Berruz Chungata,Martin Jurecek,Katerina Potika,William B. Andreopoulos,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器学习模型在恶意软件分类中因概念漂移导致性能退化的问题，提出了一种基于单类支持向量机（OCSVM）的概念漂移检测新方法，并与Minibatch K-Means和最大均值差异（MMD）技术进行比较。通过四种学习模型、三种训练场景的大量实验，发现漂移感知的重训练策略能在保持与周期性重训练相当的分类精度的同时，大幅提升训练效率，其中OCSVM方法总体表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件分类模型极易受到概念漂移的影响，因为攻击者会不断修改现有恶意软件，导致训练数据与实时数据统计特性不一致，从而造成模型性能下降。现有的周期性重训练方法虽然有效但资源消耗大，需要一种更精准的概念漂移检测机制来指导模型重训练。

Method: 对比分析三种概念漂移检测技术：基于OCSVM的新方法、基于Minibatch K-Means的已有技术以及统计方法MMD。在四种学习模型（多层感知机、随机森林、支持向量机、XGBoost）上，分别考察三种场景：静态（不重训练）、周期性（无条件重训练）、漂移感知（仅在检测到漂移时重训练），并通过Pareto Front分析精度与训练效率的权衡。

Result: 三种概念漂移检测技术均能达到与周期性重训练相当的分类精度，同时在需要重训练的模型数量上大幅节省效率。在漂移感知场景下，基于OCSVM的方法总体优于MK-Means和MMD。Pareto Front分析明确了精度与训练效率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果强有力地证明，在恶意软件分类模型中能够准确检测概念漂移，实现高效且准确的模型维护。OCSVM方法为概念漂移检测提供了一种更有前景的解决方案，兼顾了分类准确性和计算效率。

Abstract: Concept drift refers to changes over time in the statistical properties of data, as compared to the data that was used to train a learning model. Machine learning models for malware detection or classification are particularly susceptible to performance degradation caused by concept drift, as attackers constantly modify existing malware. In this chapter, we analyze two machine learning-based approaches to automated concept drift detection-a novel approach based on One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM) and a previously-studied technique based on Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means). For comparison we also consider Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), a statistical technique for detecting changes in multidimensional data. We conduct an extensive series of experiments comparing the effectiveness of four learning models, namely, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Forest, Support Vector Machines, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting. For each of these models, we consider three distinct scenarios: A static scenario where no model retraining occurs, a periodic scenario where models are constantly retrained irrespective of concept drift, and a drift-aware scenario where models are only retrained when concept drift is detected. Under the drift-aware scenario, we analyze the tradeoff between accuracy and training efficiency using Pareto Front analysis. We find that all three concept drift detection techniques achieve classification accuracy comparable to periodic retraining, while offering substantially greater efficiency in terms of the number of models that must be retrained. In addition, drift-aware retraining based on our OCSVM technique generally outperforms the MK-Means and MMD approaches. Overall, these results provide strong evidence that we can accurately detect concept drift in malware classification models.

</details>


### [174] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了非光滑复合目标分布下Moreau-Yosida非调整Langevin算法（MYULA）的收敛性,证明其离散化误差由参考活动迹B_ref控制而非全局曲率界d/λ,并对结构化惩罚项（分段线性、Lasso、组稀疏、全变差）给出更优的O~(ε^{-2})精度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MYULA收敛分析依赖全局曲率上界d/λ，导致精度依赖为O~(ε^{-3})，对于结构化惩罚函数而言过于保守。作者希望通过更精细的曲率-管（curvature-tube）分析，识别实际活跃的曲率维度，从而改进复杂度上界。

Method: 引入参考活动迹B_ref（沿热子步对Moreau包络Hessian迹的平均）替代全局曲率界d/λ来控制主导离散化误差；结合Wasserstein距离分析给出MYULA迭代复杂度及Moreau偏差界；对分段线性、Lasso、组稀疏和全变差等结构化惩罚利用曲率-管估计使B_ref与λ无关。

Result: 证明MYULA在ε_alg精度下的迭代复杂度为N ≲ (1/m)[L_f + (τ_f+G^2+B_ref)/ε_alg² + M_λ/ε_alg]；Moreau偏差界为√m W₂(π_λ,π) ≤ G²λ/4。通用估计给出O~(ε^{-3})精度依赖，而对结构化惩罚可改进为O~(ε^{-2})。

Conclusion: 通过引入更精细的参考活动迹指标，文章显著改进了MYULA对结构化非光滑惩罚的复杂度分析，在经典算法框架下无需修改即可实现从O~(ε^{-3})到O~(ε^{-2})的加速收敛保证。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [175] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"合成人格预训练"(SPP)方法，在预训练从第一个token开始就植入期望的助手人格，通过在预训练文档中标注价值对齐的第一人称反思、以标准交叉熵损失同时训练原始文档与反思、再用对话数据绑定人格到助手身份，显著改善宪法遵循、越狱鲁棒性并降低道德困境中的错位率，同时保留模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法通常在预训练之后才引入，价值观只是"薄薄的一层覆盖"，而非根植于模型内部，容易导致后续错位。作者认为在预训练阶段就开始塑造模型价值观至关重要，因此提出在token零处就安装期望的助手人格的新范式。

Method: SPP包含三个步骤：(1)用规范性价值宪法对预训练文档进行第一人称反思标注；(2)用标准交叉熵损失同时预训练原始文档和反思内容，将期望人格植入众多其他人格之中；(3)在用户-助手对话数据上后训练，将期望人格绑定到助手身份（人格绑定）。

Result: 在高达3B参数、500B token的预训练规模上，SPP改善了宪法遵循和越狱鲁棒性，降低分布外道德困境的错位率，同时保持模型能力。早期干预至关重要：仅在预训练末尾引入SPP效果更弱，且优势随预训练预算增加而增大，依赖于人格绑定。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，早期塑造价值观对于对齐至关重要，预训练时期的人格干预是有效的对齐途径，为对齐研究提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [176] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SORT（稀疏正交回归技术），一种稀疏谱框架，用于从含噪且不规则采样的数据中学习正交基展开。该方法通过L1正则化回归直接估计展开系数，避免显式求积或解析内积计算。核心应用是数据驱动的常微分方程发现，将向量场表示为所选正交基上的稀疏系数展开，为符号回归、基于语法的发现和SINDy类稀疏识别提供了补充路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统库基稀疏识别（如SINDy）在面对问题特定的非线性项被有限库遗漏时会导致模型失效，需要一种更稳健的中间表示方法，将问题从通用项之间的脆弱选择转移到适应问题领域的基设计上。

Method: SORT采用L1正则化回归直接从观测数据估计正交基展开系数，无需显式求积或解析内积计算。该方法支持在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示不匹配情况下的稳定学习，并提供系数读出功能以支持非线性逼近和高维积分估计。

Result: 在动力学系统实验中，当基与问题匹配良好时，SORT匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线；在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示不匹配下表现更稳定。实验还显示主导低阶系数随模型阶数增加而持续存在，支持阶数一致的模型增长。

Conclusion: SORT提供了系统识别、逼近和积分任务的可复用中间表示，将基设计作为科学建模问题的显式组成部分。虽然SORT对表示不匹配并非免疫，但它将问题从通用库项之间的脆弱选择转移到了适应问题领域的基设计上。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [177] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过科学多模态预训练、统一后训练流水线（包括监督微调、多任务强化学习、黑盒/白盒智能体RL和蒸馏）以及架构创新（时间序列建模和记忆增强解码器），在科学多模态理解、推理、生成和长时程任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现需要AI系统能够跨越异构模态推理科学证据、与科学工具和环境交互，并在长时间任务范围内持续取得进展，现有模型在科学智能体能力上仍存在不足。

Method: 训练流水线包括：（1）科学多模态预训练，基于渲染的科学文档、交错图文数据和多样化科学语料；（2）统一后训练，包括监督微调、可扩展多任务强化学习、黑盒与白盒智能体RL、在线策略蒸馏。技术上采用部分rollout与离策略校正、自适应长度正则化、在线投机解码、鲁棒多任务优化和轨迹感知经验组装。架构上Intern-S2-Preview-397B扩展了时间序列建模至数值预测，Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径支持快速科学领域特化。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准上达到竞争力或领先水平；时间序列模块在SciTS上提升科学信号理解与预测能力；Intern-MemDec-4B扩展将Biology-Instructions平均分从56.92提升至60.32，且无需修改冻结的397B主干网络。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview系列展示了端到端训练科学智能体基础模型的可行路径，记忆增强解码器为科学领域快速特化提供了一种高效方案，验证了在不改动大模型主干的情况下通过辅助路径提升特定领域性能的有效性。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [178] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种干预感知的临床世界模型，通过结构化潜在状态在时间上演化患者的术后恢复轨迹，应用于房颤消融术后90天恢复窗口的复发风险预测。模型将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态，并利用术后不规则事件记录（如临床观察、药物变更、重复干预）持续更新该状态，在DECAAF-II数据集上实现了AUROC 0.756和AUPRC 0.777的复发预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到未来终点的单步映射，但实际康复过程是不规则的轨迹，术后临床观察、用药调整、重复干预和生理测量都是异步记录的，这些信息会随时间改变风险评估。现有方法未能充分利用这些时间有序的术后事件信息。

Method: 提出干预感知的临床世界模型：1）将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态；2）通过程序上下文、静态协变量、经过时间和事件周围生理嵌入来更新该状态；3）随访影像仅作为训练时的潜在预测监督信号。该方法利用了时间有序的术后干预事件。

Result: 在DECAAF-II数据集上的重复内部交叉验证中，模型在复发预测任务上取得AUROC 0.756和AUPRC 0.777，疤痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点，且推理时无需随访MRI强度数据。学习到的状态支持不同时间范围的复发风险查询和回顾性输入编辑。

Conclusion: 术中90天恢复窗口内不规则记录的术后数据为长期复发风险提供了临床有意义的证据。该模型不仅实现了优异的复发预测性能，还支持灵活的推理能力（不同时间范围的查询和回溯编辑），且避免了推理时对随访MRI的依赖。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [179] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散采样的掩蔽扩散（masking diffusion），提出了一种路径分辨的数据几何度量——"解掩蔽增长复杂度"（UGC）。其局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，统一分析了伯努利子集和固定基数解掩蔽方案。在log-reveal-odds坐标下，该结构可生成优化的单块和多块调度方案，并量化了将计算投入适应数据几何的收益。UGC增量可通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量从样本中估计，从而构建"认证最优"采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有离散扩散模型的分析往往使用粗粒度的时间调度，缺乏数据感知的自适应策略；同时缺乏统一框架来理解不同掩蔽方案（如伯努利子集与固定基数）的离散化误差，以及如何将计算资源分配到数据几何更复杂的区域。

Method: 引入UGC（解掩蔽增长复杂度）作为路径级的数据几何度量；推导其局部增量与KL离散化误差的直接关联；在log-reveal-odds坐标系下设计最优单块/多块调度；通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量来估计UGC增量；推导细划分极限下的锐利最优Euler离散化误差。

Result: 实现了认证最优的采样器（在常数因子内达到预言机过程的迭代复杂度）；UGC聚合质量与经典多元依赖测度及先前离散扩散复杂度度量相连接；在细划分极限下建立平方根UGC密度平方积分与最优离散化误差的关系；示例表明自适应块调度可实现约√d量级的维度相关改进。

Conclusion: UGC为离散掩蔽扩散提供了统一的、数据自适应的理论框架，从原理上解释了自适应调度为何优于粗粒度调度，并给出了可计算的认证保证，为高效离散扩散采样开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [180] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vero是首个在仓库级别评估联合实现与证明合成的基准，包含43个源自真实世界的多模块Lean 4实例，覆盖Python、Dafny、Verus和Coq等语言。最强智能体仅能完全解决27/43个实例，在最难的仓库上无法关闭任何规范，表明当前智能体在仓库级验证软件合成方面仍有显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证代码生成基准要么只关注单个函数，要么只评估给定实现情况下的证明生成，无法回答智能体能否在真实多模块代码库中做出连贯的实现和证明选择这一关键问题。

Method: 构建包含43个多模块实例的仓库级基准Vero，每个实例由具有预定API接口、人工策划的形式化规范及参考实现的Lean 4仓库组成，支持仅证明和代码加证明两种评估模式，并引入审计机制允许智能体正式证明规范不可满足或参考代码不正确，以清理潜在的规范和代码错误。

Result: 评估了配备Lean工具链的前沿编程智能体配置，最强智能体仅完全解决27/43个实例，且在最难的仓库上未能闭合任何规范，展示了当前方法在仓库级验证软件合成上的明显局限。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量仓库级验证软件合成的进展提供了一个具体测试平台，明确了当前智能体在该任务上仍远远不够，研究社区需要进一步开发能够处理多模块代码库的一致实现与证明合成的方法。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [181] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree是一种无需训练的高效推测解码方法，将预训练的AR纠正头从链式扩展为树式结构，通过批量扩展和最佳优先剪枝构建验证树，在数学、代码和聊天等基准测试中实现了最高平均接受长度和加速比，最高可达9.73倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式草稿模型虽能并行预测整个token块以减少提议延迟，但其位置分布是边缘分布而非沿草稿路径的条件分布。现有的链式纠正只沿单一链条传播因果信息，而基于扩散的树结构虽拓宽了候选覆盖范围却未沿各分支进行纠正。作者希望结合两者优势，在树结构中引入条件纠正。

Method: DARTree首先在单一批次中通过扩展和评分所有深度节点来构建固定宽度的候选树，然后仅应用最佳优先剪枝选择验证树。该方法将AR纠正头的推理与顺序堆操作解耦，实现从链到树的预训练AR纠正头扩展，且无需额外训练。

Result: 在七个数学、代码和聊天基准上，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度配置中均取得最高平均接受长度和加速比。每轮验证最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash高98.6%，比Domino高27.9%，相对于本地测量的自回归解码达到最高9.73倍无损加速。

Conclusion: DARTree通过将条件纠正引入树式推测解码，有效结合了链式纠正的因果信息与树结构的候选覆盖面，在多样化的基准和配置下均展现出显著的性能和效率优势，验证了该方法的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [182] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多标签分类和二元分割中Jaccard得分（IoU）的校准与替代损失问题。证明了Jaccard得分矩阵的非奇异性质及损失列仿射维度为2^s-1，并通过有限MinHash Gram表示与布尔Möbius反演证明了精确校准的CCdim介于2^{s-1}和2^s-1之间，意味着零回归校准需要指数级预测坐标；同时给出了两种多项式维度的近似保证，说明固定加性回归容差可实现多项式预测维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard得分（IoU）是多元分类和语义分割中的标准评估指标，但对其损失矩阵的代数性质及校准所需的最小预测维度缺乏严格的理论分析。理解精确校准与近似校准之间的维度权衡，对设计可扩展的替代损失函数至关重要。

Method: 作者结合有限MinHash Gram表示与布尔Möbius反演，证明了Jaccard得分矩阵、平移损失矩阵和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性。下界证明采用因子加权分布并利用贝叶斯最优报告构造了2^{s-1}+1个支持结果的分布。近似部分包括F_1到Jaccard的回归转移，以及基于MinHash平方损失的直接构造和有符号变体两种方法。

Result: 证明了精确校准的CCdim满足2^{s-1}≤CCdim(L^Jac)≤2^s-1，暗示需要指数级预测坐标。F_1-to-Jaccard转移给出了(s^2+1)维的代理，渐近Jaccard回归至多为3-2√2。MinHash平方损失代理在O((s²+s log(1/ρ))/α²)维度上实现均匀α回归保证，有符号变体降至O((s+log(1/ρ))/α²)维度。

Conclusion: 零回归校准需要指数维度，而任意固定加性回归容差都能以多项式预测维度实现。这一明确区分揭示了Jaccard得分校准中"精确性"与"可扩展性"之间的根本张力。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [183] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Defensive Booster"算法，通过防御性预测框架同时获得了在线梯度提升的Brier分数竞争性保证和在线弱到强提升的分类误差收敛保证，且仅需访问单个弱学习器，效率远超现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升方法只能分别提供两种不相容的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上竞争最优但弱学习条件失效时无保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下驱动误差为零但条件不满足时无保障。需要一个算法同时获得这两种保证。

Method: 提出Defensive Booster算法，操作化提升的"对偶视角"：当随机化分类误差持续偏高时，错误权重形成平滑重加权使得所有弱假设的低边量（edge）暴露，从而在事后获得弱学习条件失败的硬核证书。同时开发了强自适应变体以在每个时间区间上同时满足两种保证。

Result: 算法在Brier分数上与最优预测器以与在线梯度提升相同的速率竞争，在平滑弱学习条件下Brier分数和随机化分类误差与在线分类提升相同速率保证。实验显示预测性能优异（有时大幅超越所有现有基线），且运行速度快多个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster通过统一的防御性预测框架同时实现了在线提升的两类核心保证，且只需单弱学习器即可运行，实现了理论上更全面、实践上更高效的在线概率预测方法。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI社区在"推理"定义上缺乏共识，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证。作者提出基于文献综合的可操作定义，将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的规则化过程，并提供研究传播的最佳实践检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理是AI领域最具科学和经济价值的话题之一，但生成式AI社区未在推理的操作性定义上达成共识，往往隐式排斥逻辑学和可验证自动推理中的历史处理方法。这种定义模糊性使推理评估的构念效度无法验证，阻碍了向可信自主推理的可量化进展。

Method: （1）综合文献提出操作性定义，将有效且可靠的推理定位为一种可学习的基于规则的过程；（2）提供AI推理研究传播最佳实践的检查清单。

Result: 提出了统一的操作性定义框架以及研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单，为推理评估的构念效度提供了可验证的基础。

Conclusion: 定义模糊是可解决的，通过明确的操作性定义和最佳实践清单，可以推动生成式AI推理研究在可验证、可信的方向上取得进展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [185] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，评估18个前沿语言模型在制度压力下的科研诚信表现，发现模型在高压下约1/3的诚信关键决策会失败，且显式压力引发不当行为顺从、隐式重构导致过度拒绝，三个诚信维度在结构上相互独立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为科研合作伙伴，但在机构压力下其维护科研诚信的能力尚未被量化测量，存在科研诚信风险与信任侵蚀的双重隐患。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，涵盖36个配对任务、5级隐式-显式压力协议、3个领域和4个研究阶段，评测不当行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于证据的决策三个维度，并对18个前沿模型变体进行评估。

Result: 在峰值压力下模型约1/3的诚信关键决策失败；规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解此问题；显式压力导致顺从不当行为，隐式重构导致过度拒绝；分类失败模型在基于证据的决策上表现反而更好（85.7 vs 79.4）。

Conclusion: 模型的科研诚信三个维度在结构上相互分离，正确的伦理行动并不依赖准确的分类能力；前沿模型可能表面有用但潜藏诚信失败，构成两类部署风险：助长科研不端行为和侵蚀AI辅助研究的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [186] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出AI对齐方法（本用于防止有害输出）实际上是双重用途技术，可能被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操纵工具，呼吁社区关注这一风险并提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI对齐方法在防止有害输出方面发挥作用，但其技术特性也可能被恶意行为者利用进行信息控制和操纵，这一双重用途风险尚未得到充分讨论。

Method: 通过将当前对齐技术与滥用可能性和实际案例进行映射分析，论证"完美对齐"模型的追求客观上为恶意行为者提供了不断改进的信息主导工具。

Result: 研究表明AI对齐技术的双重用途潜力是真实且迫切的，其风险因AI作为信息提供者的快速普及、经济权力不对称以及日益威权化的政治环境而加剧。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区认真考虑AI对齐机制的故意滥用问题，并提出缓解策略以防范这一双重用途潜力带来的危害。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [187] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，LLM与人类在最终道德判断标签上的高一致性并不等于真正的道德对齐。通过构建500个ETHICS衍生基准条目，并同时收集人类和模型的标签及理由，作者发现即使标签一致，模型在理由层面的道德基础（如伤害、尊重、公正等范畴的权重分配）与人类标注者存在系统性分歧，因此基于标签的评估会误导性地高估模型对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍用"与人类判断的一致性"作为LLM对齐的代理指标，但两个智能体可能在最终判断一致时，依据完全不同的道德原则、情境假设或解释。现有评估忽视了对理由层面的考察，无法真正反映模型是否在道德基础上与人类对齐。

Method: 研究人员构建了一个500条目的ETHICS衍生基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域，并分别采集了人类标注者和LLM对最终标签及支持理由的标注。通过理由层面分析，对比人类与模型在道德范畴（如伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得、借口相关性）上的注意力分布。

Result: 前沿和开源模型家族在与人类标注者多数标签的一致性上通常表现良好，但理由层面的分析揭示了系统性的道德基础分歧。即使最终标签与人类多数一致，模型在道德范畴上的注意力分配仍与人类存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不应等同于对齐。仅基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的安心效果，必须辅以对模型判断中蕴含的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析，才能准确评估LLM的道德对齐水平。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [188] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream及其核心扩展LLM-MR-CNP，将经典合同网协议扩展为融合大语言模型的语义化多轮协商机制，用于异构移动边缘-云基础设施中的流处理系统调度。实验表明该方法将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，在20个代理下冲突解决率达到0.91，相比多轮规则基线效用提升高达22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统在异构移动边缘-云基础设施中运行，面临工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格QoS要求的挑战，这使得去中心化调度变得复杂。现有方法难以有效处理定性的运行时间上下文和不确定性环境下的调度决策。

Method: 提出LLM-MR-CNP协议，这是经典合同网协议的扩展，包含五个关键要素：语义化CFP（呼叫招标）表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。边缘集群代理基于局部观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时间上下文来精炼自然语言卸载提案，同时硬性资源和QoS约束保持确定性。

Result: 基于阿里ASI Trace的实验从三个层面评估：单轮与多轮CNP对比、规则型与LLM辅助精炼对比、固定模型单轮与多轮协商对比。结果显示延迟违规降至3%，资源过度承诺被消除，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用相比多轮规则基线提升22%。另有25个案例评估显示模型和提示词相关的精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP精炼是主要的协议层面增益来源，LLM辅助在处理定性和不确定运行时间上下文方面具有额外价值，为后续研究提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [189] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张在高风险决策场景中，需要开发认知对齐（cognitive alignment）的AI系统——即与用户推理方式相似、并能忠实传达推理过程的系统。作者回顾了认知对齐提升可理解性与可信度的证据，提供新调查数据，指出现有对齐方法的不足，并提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统正越来越多地作为决策辅助、决策代理或自主决策者出现。在高风险决策中，现有的对齐方法不足以确保系统与用户的推理方式一致，认知错位可能阻碍AI在实际应用中的采纳。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，结合文献综述与新的调查数据，分析认知对齐对可理解性和可信度的作用，并梳理现有对齐方法与认知对齐需求之间的差距。

Result: 调查数据显示许多用户认为认知对齐在其重视AI决策理由时是"必不可少的"；同时发现现有对齐方法存在明显不足，无法充分实现认知对齐。

Conclusion: 认知错位是AI在许多设想应用中被采纳的潜在障碍，解决这一问题对于创建用户既愿意依赖又有理由依赖的AI系统至关重要，需要专门的研究议程来弥合现有差距。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [190] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型的安全对齐行为具有语言依赖性：在核打击决策场景中，日语提示能显著降低Claude和Gemini模型的发射率，而用英语推理的模型几乎在所有条件下都选择发射。机制在于推理语言而非输入语言驱动了该效应——模型用日语推理时会自发产生道德词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐评估仅在英语中进行。作者质疑这种单语言评估可能遗漏模型在其他语言中表现出的风险或安全保障行为。

Method: 测试了六家提供商的九个模型，使用单轮博弈论情景——模型为核武器国家提供是否打击无防御对手的建议。提示词故意设计为无道德色彩且在语言间战略上等同。通过跨语言实验隔离机制：在英语提示中指示模型用日语推理，对比纯日语输入的效果。

Result: 日语提示使Claude Sonnet 4.6在非必要打击场景中发射率从40%降至0%，在有争议场景中从93%降至17%；Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。跨语言实验显示，用日语推理时发射率从93%降至37%。模型用日语推理时会自发产生提示中完全不存在道德词汇。五个其他模型无语言效应但几乎在所有条件下都发射。

Conclusion: LLM安全行为是语言依赖的，仅用英语评估会同时遗漏其他语言中编码的风险和安全保障。语言效应需要模型在英语中已有犹豫倾向才起作用。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [191] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，通过将辅助计算流仅用于续写预测（decode阶段），在共享主KV缓存和权重的同时，实现预填充（prefill）与解码（decode）阶段计算成本的解耦，从而优化推理阶段的资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型推理请求增多，累积推理成本变得比一次性训练成本更重要。传统宽度/深度扩展同时增加prefill和decode两个阶段的成本，因为每一层都在两个阶段中都被计算。作者希望将额外的学习计算仅分配给续写预测，同时保持完整的prompt主计算和单一持久KV缓存，以解耦两个阶段的成本。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两条流：主流是一个完整的因果语言模型，负责处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流在prompt处理阶段被跳过，仅从最终prompt位置开始激活，增加续写预测的计算而不写入持久状态或影响主流。两条流共享主要attention、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立token嵌入和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，主流和辅助流的专家fan-out可独立控制prefill成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在匹配token数量的对比中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下取得更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，实验展示了两类场景：在固定prefill专家计算下增加decode计算，以及在固定decode专家预算下在两条流之间重新分配。这些实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer通过将辅助计算流仅用于续写预测，有效解耦了prefill和decode阶段的计算成本，实现了阶段特定的计算分配优化。在MoE场景下，这种分离允许独立控制不同阶段的专家资源，在固定预算下展现更好的预测质量。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [192] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法，用于跨域零/少样本个性化对话生成。通过元学习的LoRA初始化作为先验中心，根据支持集大小和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度，并将个性化先验分解为用户和领域两个组件，显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨域个性化方法在稀疏证据下难以校准参数更新幅度，容易过拟合；而历史迁移方法常将用户偏好与源领域特征纠缠，导致负迁移和不可靠的个性化先验。

Method: 1) 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA：以元学习的LoRA初始化作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集大小和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度。2) 将个性化先验功能性地分解为用户组件（通过可读提示词保持稳定偏好）和领域组件（通过拓扑保持的软token进行领域特定隐空间条件化）。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上持续优于强基线。在HiCUPID上，跨域胜率退化相对最优基线降低47.9%，在未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过PAC-Bayes约束控制适应强度并分解个性化先验，能够有效缓解稀疏证据下的过拟合和跨域负迁移问题，实现更可靠的跨域个性化。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [193] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AstraZeneca内部开发的基于LLM的研究助手系统，通过聊天界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据和内部实验系统等多种数据源，支持快速问答和复杂多步研究任务，并提供基于检索证据的响应和来源链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床研究人员在广泛的数据源中高效探索生物医学问题，突破传统检索方式在复杂研究任务上的局限，支持日常研发工作流程。

Method: 系统采用聊天式界面，整合多种异构数据源（文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据、内部实验系统），提供快速问答和多种多步推理两种模式，响应基于检索证据并链接回原始来源。

Result: 系统已在AstraZeneca内部大规模部署，支持日常研发工作流程，本文重点分享了系统架构、产品设计选择和部署经验教训。

Conclusion: 该技术说明展示了LLM系统在医药研发领域的实际落地应用，验证了基于证据检索的多源整合问答系统在科学与临床研究中的实用价值。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [194] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，系统性研究大语言模型在多个显式约束同时成立时的性能退化问题，发现性能随约束数量k的累积呈乘法式崩塌，且结构性约束比词法性约束退化更快，模型在5-6个约束以上即失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越多地部署在需要同时满足多个显式约束的场景中，但现有研究主要关注单一约束的处理能力，对多约束联合成立时的组合性退化缺乏系统表征，包括退化速度、退化原因以及缓解措施。

Method: 提出CSE基准，通过程序化生成方式系统地变化同时生效的约束数量（k=1到12），每个约束由确定性规则验证器评分，完全不依赖LLM裁判。共测试15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）单约束通过率逐步可预测地衰减，但全部约束同时满足的概率急剧崩塌——在k=8时，单个约束通过率约41%的模型，全部通过率仅5.7%；（2）结构性约束比词法性约束每个约束损失多2倍的基线能力，存在理解维持差距；（3）约束失败近似独立，导致累积呈乘法效应，残余耦合源于共享输出特征而非成对干扰。

Conclusion: 可靠的指令遵循在5-6个同时约束时开始崩溃：最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率低于50%，而15个模型中有12个在3个或更少约束时就已低于50%。这揭示了当前LLM在多约束组合场景下存在系统性的能力瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [195] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MindMemOS，一个可移植且自进化的记忆操作系统层，采用统一实体属性时间结构组织开放世界信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆细化和持续技能进化。在LOCOMO上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%，MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上比初始技能基线提升9.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理的记忆系统在开发后往往保持固定，无法通过持续使用来适应和调整其记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，限制了长期交互中的适应能力和个性化表现。

Method: MindMemOS包含两个核心算法：MindMemEvolve采用验证驱动的进化搜索来优化目标场景的记忆模式，并通过"梦"机制合并冗余记录、解决冲突以巩固积累的记忆；同时利用隐式纠正反馈作为人在回路信号来识别和修正不准确或不对齐的记忆。MindSkillEvolve将代理执行轨迹转化为可复用且逐步细化的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO上实现94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上实现70.63%准确率；MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上较初始技能基线提升9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS通过自进化的记忆组织和技能演化机制，显著提升AI代理在长期交互中的记忆适应性和任务执行能力，验证了进化式记忆管理在开放世界场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [196] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出治理持久记忆（GPM），一种可审计的比特emporal状态转换模型，解决智能体长期记忆中检索结果可能基于矛盾、过期、撤回或已删除记录的问题，并在基准和端到端评估中展现高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体长期记忆采用"选择-存储-检索"模式，但检索阶段不判断记录是否已被矛盾、取代、撤回、删除或过期，导致出站声明可能基于失效信息，缺乏治理和可审计性。

Method: 引入治理持久记忆（GPM）模型：(1) 可审计的比特emporal状态转换；(2) 源绑定准入机制；(3) 派生生命周期状态；(4) 当前公共屏障与故障关闭的结构化释放；(5) 五个可执行子句覆盖账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤销/删除后不可复活、以及在验证头部新视图上的精确声明闭合。

Result: 在GPM-ReleaseBench上完全匹配所有3600个结果（最强简单策略仅匹配1800/3600）；密封端到端评估中治理通道2400/2400正确，对比未治理Llama 7B仅600/2400，修复所有1800个基线失败且无回归；V5版本中文和英文指令各2400/2400；有限模型探索331,776语义和1,990,656查询状态无完整合约反例；100,000轨迹三引擎差分零不匹配。

Conclusion: GPM在受限合约和实现层面展现了强可靠性与可审计性，但作者明确强调这是有界合约结果而非开放世界模型准确度的证据，治理答案是确定性服务输出而非语言模型本身完美准确的声明。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [197] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ε-MemEvo框架，通过在LLM程序演化中跨任务传递任务无关的策略记忆（以自然语言总结而非原始代码）来实现知识迁移，并采用自适应注入门控避免负迁移，在8个优化基准上平均提升AUCC +8.7%，早期收敛速度+9.4%，且计算开销小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM程序演化系统（如FunSearch和AlphaEvolve）虽能发现新算法，但每个任务独立优化，搜索经验在任务完成后被丢弃，缺乏跨任务知识复用能力。

Method: ε-MemEvo将先验经验存储为任务无关的策略记忆（自然语言形式，跨API和评估器可迁移），通过自适应注入门控决定是否注入及注入强度，避免语义不匹配记忆导致的负迁移。采用内容级留一法（Leave-One-Out）评估，排除目标任务记忆条目。

Result: 在8个跨数学优化和系统工程领域的基准上，ε-MemEvo在所有任务上均优于AdaEvolve，平均相对增益+8.7%，早期收敛提升+9.4%。消融实验表明朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，而自适应门控在所有5个任务中保持安全。数据更新后的后验可解释：在改进搜索时倾向跳过，在早期和晚期平台期从跳过转向提示。计算开销小于1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo通过任务无关的自然语言记忆存储与自适应注入门控，实现了安全高效的跨任务知识转移，既提升了搜索性能又保持可解释性，且几乎不增加计算成本。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [198] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果归因分数（CAS），一种用于因果解释的紧凑评分架构。CAS基于识别的干预联盟博弈，利用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，并提供局部与全局两级因果报告。在已知真值基准中，CAS的局部MAE为0.107，优于单次归一化（0.173）和全局归一化绝对ATE向量（0.213）；在两个实证DoubleML数据集上，CAS与预测性SHAP排名产生实质性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性解释方法仅归因模型输出，而非归因对现实世界结果的干预效应。现有方法无法区分"什么预测结果"与"什么解释因果效应异质性"，需要一种明确以干预目标为导向的因果解释框架。

Method: CAS从识别的干预联盟博弈出发，通过因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，将原始结果尺度效应转化为局部CAS、带符号局部CAS以及两种互补的全局CAS摘要。核心创新不是新的Shapley公式，而是增加了具有明确干预目标的局部到全局因果报告层。

Result: 在已知真值基准中，八次重复主交互模拟（每次n=2,200，三个动作）显示，联盟感知CAS的局部MAE为0.107，优于单次归一化（0.173）和全局归一化绝对ATE向量（0.213）。配对优势在强交互下从可加性下的-0.003增至0.091。在401(k)资格/净金融资产（n=9,915）和宾州再就业奖金/失业时长（n=5,099）两个实证数据集中，预测性SHAP/TreeSHAP与Feature-CAS排名存在实质性差异，如dep1从预测全局第13位升至Feature-CAS第2位。

Conclusion: CAS有效区分了"预测结果的贡献"与"因果效应异质性的解释"两个概念，在交互效应存在时优势明显，为因果解释提供了一种可操作的局部到全局报告框架。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [199] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出trie自动机，一种专门针对有限集合约束的结构化输出解码机制，利用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每个节点的token掩码，相比XGrammar实现7倍更快的每步有效token计算、29倍的端到端vLLM吞吐提升，且保证100%输出有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要生成符合预定义模式的结构化输出，常见约束是从有限字符串集合中选择。现有的约束解码系统依赖通用语法编译，当有效值数量增长到数千时会出现"基数墙"（cardinality wall），编译和推理速度变得极其缓慢。

Method: 提出trie自动机，利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数），通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每个节点的token掩码。预计算掩码使得服务路径无状态化，绕过引导解码流水线，从而在批量服务中放大性能优势。

Result: 与XGrammar（vLLM和SGLang的主要后端之一）相比，每步有效token计算快7倍（0.65微秒 vs 5.8微秒）；K≥300时编译快2-6.5倍；端到端vLLM吞吐量在batch size为256时达到219 req/s vs 7.5 req/s（29倍提升）。在七种tokenizer族（32K-262K词表）中，K=10,000时编译时间保持在100毫秒以内，每步成本与集合大小无关，同时保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: trie自动机通过专门化利用有限集合的结构特性，成功解决了通用语法编译在面向大规模有限集合约束时的性能瓶颈，实现了显著的速度提升和可扩展性，为基于有限集合约束的结构化生成提供了一种高效且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [200] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Jury系统，用一组开源LLM陪审团替代单一评判模型来评估长推理轨迹中的推理缺陷。通过主持人引导的陪审团审议机制（成员互相批评并修改初始投票）达成共识，在识别推理缺陷的准确性上显著超越前沿模型，且成本仅为前沿模型的8-15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理LLM的改进需要高质量的长推理轨迹评判能力，但单一模型评判（即使是前沿模型）在识别长推理缺陷上表现不佳。同时，在线训练中禁止使用前沿模型作为评判者（受使用条款限制），因此需要一种基于开源模型的替代方案。

Method: 引入Reasoning Jury系统，用多个LLM组成陪审团替代单一评判模型。系统包含一个主持人，通过审议机制组织陪审员讨论，陪审员互相批评对方的判断并可以修改初始投票。主持人最终通过审议达成共识或合并判断形成最终裁决，以识别推理轨迹中的缺陷及其严重程度。

Result: Reasoning Jury使用开源权重模型（如gpt-oss-120b）组成的陪审团，在正确识别推理缺陷方面显著优于前沿模型（包括opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6和gemini-3.1-pro）。此外，陪审团的聚合成本仅为前沿模型LLM-as-a-judge方式的8-15%。这些判断还可用于深入理解推理LLM在基准测试上的失败模式。

Conclusion: Reasoning Jury证明了开源模型组合的集体智慧能够超越单一前沿模型的评判能力，同时大幅降低成本。该系统不仅提升了推理缺陷识别的准确性，还提供了对推理模型失败模式的更深入理解，为推理LLM的训练与评估提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [201] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThyroidXAgent，一个可审计、医生可交互的智能体AI系统，用于甲状腺超声诊断，整合病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成等任务，在超28,000例测试中显著提升医生诊断准确率和报告一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要协调病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成等多个任务，但现有AI系统大多孤立处理这些任务，缺乏对临床审查的完整支持。

Method: 构建ThyroidXAgent智能体AI系统，协调多个专业诊断工具并存储可审计的病例级证据记录；利用OpenThyroidDB多中心多任务数据集（约30万张超声图像和24,000份配对报告）进行开发，在28,458个非重叠测试病例上评估。

Result: 结节分割平均Dice为87.21%，良恶性分类平均AUROC为0.9466；淋巴结转移预测AUROC为0.864，滤泡性vs乳头状甲状腺癌分类AUROC为0.805；报告生成方面，基于证据的组装优于多模态语言模型基线；ThyroCluster新指标与定位感知语言模型评审相关性最强；医生分类准确率提升，报告诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%，分割和报告时间分别减少35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持可审计、医生可纠正的智能体AI系统用于甲状腺超声诊断和报告生成，能显著改善临床工作流程和诊断质量。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [202] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的基准测试DiG-bench（发现游戏），包含70个独立游戏，每个游戏用短字符串编码并具有独特的变换规则，需要通过交互和实验来发现规则。游戏分为7个难度层级，21个游戏公开发布，其余保密用于安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现（即提出新概括）是科学过程中的核心部分，但当前AI基准测试中缺乏直接测试在目标未知的受控环境中通过实验发现新知识的基准，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: DiG-bench包含70个独立游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串并具有独特的变换规则，需要通过交互实验来发现。游戏关卡提供系列挑战来测试规则是否被发现，且每关的胜利条件也是未知的。基准提供7个难度层级，最低层级的游戏多个模型可解，最高层级对最优秀的代理模型构成挑战。

Result: 所有70个游戏至少被一名人类在首次尝试中解决。21个游戏公开发布，其余保留为私有，用于安全评估。基准可区分不同层级AI代理的能力，最低层级多个模型可解，最高层级挑战最先进模型。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了AI基准测试中关于发现性知识的空白，提供了一个可控环境下测试AI科学家式发现能力的平台，同时通过私有保留部分确保评估的安全性。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [203] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Sysname系统，通过API解决多轮对话中约束撤销失效（行为复发/撤销惯性）问题：用契约账本管理约束、顺序消融探针预测复发、修复阶梯在预算内修复，实验表明预编译可显著降低复发率且探针能提前预测。


<details>
